// ============================================================================
// FILE: /supabase/functions/checkout/index.ts
// NEW FILE
// ============================================================================
// SYD OMEGA 91717 -- Stripe Checkout (DORMANT until the founder enables payments)
// Double-gated: refuses unless payments_enabled=true AND STRIPE_SECRET_KEY is set.
// Env (Supabase secrets): STRIPE_SECRET_KEY, STRIPE_PRICE_MAP (JSON tier->price_id),
//   SITE_URL, SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY, SUPABASE_ANON_KEY
import { createClient } from "https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2";

const cors = {
  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, content-type",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "POST, OPTIONS",
};
const json = (b: unknown, s = 200) =>
  new Response(JSON.stringify(b), { status: s, headers: { ...cors, "Content-Type": "application/json" } });

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response("ok", { headers: cors });
  try {
    const url = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!;
    const svc = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")!;
    const admin = createClient(url, svc);

    // GATE 1 -- the founder's feature flag
    const { data: flag } = await admin.rpc("get_platform_flag", { p_key: "payments_enabled" });
    if (flag !== true) return json({ enabled: false, message: "Payments are not active yet." });

    // GATE 2 -- the Stripe secret must be configured
    const sk = Deno.env.get("STRIPE_SECRET_KEY");
    if (!sk) return json({ enabled: true, configured: false, message: "Payment processor not configured." });

    // identify the member from their JWT
    const jwt = (req.headers.get("Authorization") || "").replace("Bearer ", "");
    const { data: u } = await createClient(url, Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY")!, {
      global: { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${jwt}` } },
    }).auth.getUser();
    if (!u?.user) return json({ error: "not_authenticated" }, 401);

    const { tier } = await req.json().catch(() => ({ tier: "" }));
    const priceMap = JSON.parse(Deno.env.get("STRIPE_PRICE_MAP") || "{}");
    const price = priceMap[tier];
    if (!price) return json({ error: "unknown_tier" }, 400);

    // create a Stripe Checkout Session (subscription) via the REST API
    const site = Deno.env.get("SITE_URL") || "https://www.sydomega.com";
    const form = new URLSearchParams();
    form.set("mode", "subscription");
    form.set("line_items[0][price]", price);
    form.set("line_items[0][quantity]", "1");
    form.set("success_url", `${site}/subscriptions.html?checkout=success`);
    form.set("cancel_url", `${site}/subscriptions.html?checkout=cancel`);
    form.set("customer_email", u.user.email || "");
    form.set("client_reference_id", u.user.id);
    form.set("metadata[uid]", u.user.id);
    form.set("metadata[tier]", tier);
    form.set("subscription_data[metadata][uid]", u.user.id);
    form.set("subscription_data[metadata][tier]", tier);

    const r = await fetch("https://api.stripe.com/v1/checkout/sessions", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${sk}`, "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
      body: form,
    });
    const session = await r.json();
    if (!r.ok) return json({ error: "stripe_error", detail: session?.error?.message }, 400);
    return json({ url: session.url });
  } catch (e) {
    return json({ error: String(e) }, 500);
  }
});

// ============================================================================
// FILE: /supabase/functions/stripe-webhook/index.ts
// NEW FILE
// ============================================================================
// SYD OMEGA 91717 -- Stripe Webhook: records subscription results onto the member.
// Env: STRIPE_SECRET_KEY, STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET, SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY
import Stripe from "https://esm.sh/stripe@14?target=deno";
import { createClient } from "https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2";

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  const sk = Deno.env.get("STRIPE_SECRET_KEY");
  const wh = Deno.env.get("STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET");
  if (!sk || !wh) return new Response("not configured", { status: 503 });

  const stripe = new Stripe(sk, { apiVersion: "2024-06-20" });
  const sig = req.headers.get("stripe-signature") || "";
  const body = await req.text();

  let event: Stripe.Event;
  try {
    event = await stripe.webhooks.constructEventAsync(body, sig, wh);
  } catch (e) {
    return new Response(`bad signature: ${e}`, { status: 400 });
  }

  const admin = createClient(Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!, Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")!);
  const apply = (uid: string, tier: string, status: string, end: number | null, cust: string | null) =>
    admin.rpc("apply_subscription", {
      p_uid: uid, p_tier: tier, p_status: status,
      p_period_end: end ? new Date(end * 1000).toISOString() : null, p_customer: cust,
    });

  try {
    if (event.type === "checkout.session.completed") {
      const s = event.data.object as Stripe.Checkout.Session;
      const uid = s.client_reference_id || (s.metadata?.uid ?? "");
      const tier = s.metadata?.tier ?? "";
      if (uid) await apply(uid, tier, "active", null, (s.customer as string) ?? null);
    } else if (event.type.startsWith("customer.subscription.")) {
      const sub = event.data.object as Stripe.Subscription;
      const uid = (sub.metadata?.uid ?? "");
      const tier = (sub.metadata?.tier ?? "");
      const status = event.type.endsWith("deleted") ? "canceled" : sub.status;
      if (uid) await apply(uid, tier, status, sub.current_period_end ?? null, (sub.customer as string) ?? null);
    }
  } catch (_) { /* swallow -- Stripe will retry */ }

  return new Response(JSON.stringify({ received: true }), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
});

<script type="module">
  import { createClient } from 'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2';
  const supabase = createClient("https://ydqhzvvoyufiiqvzcjns.supabase.co","sb_publishable_9KlhhnvRs4OKgw6nxXHmYw_GxszJ46q");
  let user=null, axisA=1, cleared={}, selD=-1;

  const DISC=[['Aries','Ares'],['Taurus','Aphrodite'],['Gemini','Hermes'],['Cancer','Artemis'],['Leo','Apollo'],['Virgo','Athena'],['Libra','Hera'],['Scorpio','Demeter'],['Sagittarius','Zeus'],['Capricorn','Hestia'],['Aquarius','Hephaestus'],['Pisces','Poseidon']];
  const ROMAN=['I','II','III','IV','V','VI','VII','VIII','IX','X','XI','XII'];
  function key(d,l){ return 'd'+d+'l'+l; }
  function isCleared(d,l){ return !!cleared[key(d,l)]; }
  function clearsIn(d){ var n=0; for(var l=0;l<12;l++) if(isCleared(d,l)) n++; return n; }
  function discUnlocked(d){ if(d===0) return true; return clearsIn(d-1)>=6 && axisA>=d; }
  function lessonUnlocked(d,l){ if(!discUnlocked(d)) return false; return l===0 || isCleared(d,l-1); }

  function rnd(n){ return Math.floor(Math.random()*n); }
  function node(){ return [1+rnd(9),1+rnd(9),1+rnd(9)]; }
  function auth(n){ return Math.sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]+n[2]*n[2]); }
  function ns(n){ return '('+n[0]+','+n[1]+','+n[2]+')'; }
  function shuffle(a){ for(var i=a.length-1;i>0;i--){ var j=rnd(i+1); var t=a[i];a[i]=a[j];a[j]=t; } return a; }
  function opts(correct, distractors){
    var set=[String(correct)]; var guard=0;
    while(set.length<4 && guard<50){ var d=distractors(); var s=String(d); if(set.indexOf(s)===-1) set.push(s); guard++; }
    return shuffle(set.map(function(s){ return {label:s, correct:(s===String(correct))}; }));
  }

  function genTier(){ var n=node(); var c=Math.min(n[0],n[1],n[2]); return {text:'Tier of node '+ns(n)+'  --  the floor of its lowest axis?', options:opts(c,function(){return 1+rnd(9);})}; }
  function genAuth(){ var n=node(); var c=auth(n).toFixed(2); return {text:'Authority of node '+ns(n)+' = \u221a(A\u00b2+B\u00b2+C\u00b2), to 2 decimals?', options:opts(c,function(){return (auth(n)+(rnd(7)-3)*0.37).toFixed(2);})}; }
  function genBelow(){ var n=node(); var c=(n[0]-1)*(n[1]-1)*(n[2]-1); return {text:'How many nodes lie strictly below '+ns(n)+' (all three coordinates smaller)?', options:opts(c,function(){return Math.max(0,c+(rnd(9)-4)*((rnd(3)+1)));})}; }
  function genHighest(k){ var arr=[]; for(var i=0;i<k;i++) arr.push(node()); var best=0; for(var i=1;i<k;i++) if(auth(arr[i])>auth(arr[best])) best=i;
    var o=arr.map(function(n,i){ return {label:ns(n), correct:i===best}; }); return {text:'Which node is the most ascended (highest authority)?', options:shuffle(o)}; }

  function question(d){
    var pool;
    if(d<12) pool=[genTier, function(){return genHighest(2);}];
    else if(d<24) pool=[genTier, genAuth, function(){return genHighest(3);}];
    else if(d<36) pool=[genAuth, genBelow, function(){return genHighest(4);}];
    else pool=[genAuth, genBelow, function(){return genHighest(4);}, genTier];
    return pool[rnd(pool.length)]();
  }

  function sleep(ms){ return new Promise(function(r){ setTimeout(r,ms); }); }
  function showGate(h,p){ document.getElementById('gate-h').textContent=h; document.getElementById('gate-p').textContent=p; document.getElementById('gate').style.display='block'; document.getElementById('app').style.display='none'; }

  function renderDisc(){
    var box=document.getElementById('games'); box.innerHTML='';
    DISC.forEach(function(gm,d){
      var unlocked=discUnlocked(d), n=clearsIn(d);
      var el=document.createElement('div'); el.className='game'+(unlocked?'':' locked')+(d===selD?' sel':'');
      var lock=unlocked?'':'<div class="lock">LOCKED  .  need Knowledge \u2265 '+d+' and 6 lessons cleared in '+ROMAN[d-1]+'</div>';
      el.innerHTML='<div class="gi">DISCIPLINE '+ROMAN[d]+' \u00B7 '+gm[0].toUpperCase()+'</div><div class="gn">'+gm[1]+'\u2019s Codex</div><div class="gs">'+n+' / 12 CLEARED</div><div class="bar"><i style="width:'+(n/12*100)+'%"></i></div>'+lock;
      if(unlocked) el.addEventListener('click',function(){ openDisc(d); });
      box.appendChild(el);
    });
  }
  function openDisc(d){
    selD=d; renderDisc();
    var st=document.getElementById('stages'); st.classList.add('open');
    document.getElementById('st-name').textContent=DISC[d][1]+'\u2019s Codex \u2014 Discipline '+ROMAN[d];
    document.getElementById('st-sub').textContent='Answer each lesson without error. One wrong reading fails it.';
    var nb=document.getElementById('st-nodes'); nb.innerHTML='';
    for(var l=0;l<12;l++){ (function(l){
      var cl=isCleared(d,l), avail=lessonUnlocked(d,l);
      var n=document.createElement('div'); n.className='node'+(cl?' cleared':(avail&&!cl?' avail':''));
      n.textContent=ROMAN[l];
      if(avail) n.addEventListener('click',function(){ launch(d,l); });
      nb.appendChild(n);
    })(l); }
    st.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'nearest'});
  }

  // ===== lesson challenge =====
  let curD=-1,curL=-1,need=1,got=0,locked=false;
  function launch(d,l){
    curD=d; curL=l; var diff=d*12+l;
    need=Math.min(9, 1+Math.floor(diff/4));
    got=0; locked=false;
    document.getElementById('m-title').textContent='DISCIPLINE '+ROMAN[d]+' \u00B7 LESSON '+ROMAN[l];
    document.getElementById('m-diff').textContent='ANSWER '+need+' IN A ROW \u00B7 NO ERRORS'+(isCleared(d,l)?' \u00B7 REPLAY':' \u00B7 NEW NODE');
    document.getElementById('modal').classList.add('open');
    nextQ();
  }
  function nextQ(){
    locked=false;
    var q=question(curD*12+curL);
    document.getElementById('m-stat').textContent='';
    document.getElementById('m-q').textContent=q.text;
    document.getElementById('m-q').setAttribute('data-q',(got+1)+'/'+need);
    var ob=document.getElementById('m-opts'); ob.innerHTML='';
    document.getElementById('m-stat').textContent='Question '+(got+1)+' of '+need;
    q.options.forEach(function(o){
      var b=document.createElement('button'); b.className='opt'; b.textContent=o.label;
      b.addEventListener('click',function(){ choose(b,o,q); });
      ob.appendChild(b);
    });
  }
  async function choose(btn,o,q){
    if(locked) return; locked=true;
    if(o.correct){
      btn.classList.add('good');
      got++;
      if(got>=need){ await clearLesson(); return; }
      await sleep(420); nextQ();
    } else {
      btn.classList.add('bad');
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.opt'),function(el){ if(el.textContent===String(q.options.filter(function(x){return x.correct;})[0].label)) el.classList.add('good'); });
      document.getElementById('m-stat').textContent='WRONG READING \u2014 the lesson resets.';
      document.getElementById('m-row').innerHTML='<button class="mbtn" id="m-retry" data-i18n="ui.retry">RETRY</button><button class="mbtn ghost" id="m-close2">LEAVE</button>';
      document.getElementById('m-retry').addEventListener('click',function(){ got=0; document.getElementById('m-row').innerHTML='<button class="mbtn ghost" id="m-close">LEAVE</button>'; document.getElementById('m-close')?.addEventListener('click',closeModal); nextQ(); });
      document.getElementById('m-close2').addEventListener('click',closeModal);
    }
  }
  async function clearLesson(){
    document.getElementById('m-stat').textContent='LESSON CLEARED \u2014 awarding Knowledge...';
    document.getElementById('m-opts').innerHTML='';
    var already=isCleared(curD,curL);
    cleared[key(curD,curL)]=true;
    try{ await supabase.rpc('complete_task',{p_kind:'academy',p_task:key(curD,curL),p_axis:'a',p_title:'Discipline '+(curD+1)+' Lesson '+(curL+1)}); }catch(e){}
    if(!already) axisA=Math.min(9, axisA+0.25);
    document.getElementById('m-stat').textContent=already?'NODE ALREADY YOURS \u2014 sharp as ever.':'NODE CLEARED \u2014 +0.25 KNOWLEDGE.';
    document.getElementById('m-row').innerHTML='<button class="mbtn" id="m-cont" data-i18n="ui.continue_btn">CONTINUE</button>';
    document.getElementById('m-cont').addEventListener('click',closeModal);
    renderDisc(); openDisc(curD); updateMStat();
  }
  function closeModal(){ document.getElementById('modal').classList.remove('open'); document.getElementById('m-row').innerHTML='<button class="mbtn ghost" id="m-close">LEAVE</button>'; document.getElementById('m-close')?.addEventListener('click',closeModal); }

  function updateMStat(){ var total=0; for(var d=0;d<12;d++) total+=clearsIn(d); document.getElementById('mstat').innerHTML='KNOWLEDGE (A) <b>'+axisA.toFixed(2)+'</b> / 9 \u00B7 NODES CLEARED <b>'+total+'</b> / 144'; }

  async function boot(){
    var sess=(await supabase.auth.getSession()).data.session;
    if(!sess){ showGate('The Academy','Sign in to enter the Academy.'); return; }
    user=sess.user;
    var pr=await supabase.from('profiles').select('sign,axis_a').eq('id',user.id).maybeSingle();
    if(!pr.data || !pr.data.sign){ showGate('Set Your Cosmology','Set your cosmology first to enter the Academy.'); return; }
    axisA=Number(pr.data.axis_a||1);
    try{ var tc=await supabase.from('task_completions').select('task').eq('user_id',user.id).eq('kind','academy'); if(tc.data) tc.data.forEach(function(r){ cleared[r.task]=true; }); }catch(e){}
    document.getElementById('gate').style.display='none';
    document.getElementById('app').style.display='flex';
    renderDisc(); updateMStat();
  }
  document.getElementById('m-close')?.addEventListener('click',closeModal);
  document.getElementById('logout')?.addEventListener('click',async function(){ await supabase.auth.signOut(); window.location.href='/account.html'; });
  boot();
</script>

  function signOf(m,d){
    const b=[[1,19,'Capricorn'],[2,18,'Aquarius'],[3,20,'Pisces'],[4,19,'Aries'],[5,20,'Taurus'],[6,20,'Gemini'],[7,22,'Cancer'],[8,22,'Leo'],[9,22,'Virgo'],[10,22,'Libra'],[11,21,'Scorpio'],[12,21,'Sagittarius']];

    return 'Capricorn';
  }
  function find(n){ return SIGNS.find(s=>s[0]===n); }
  const MATS=['Sand','Glass','Iron','Steel','Titanium','Carbon','Gold','Platinum','Omega Master'];
  const ELEM_ACCENT={Fire:'#E86A3A',Water:'#34C6E6',Wind:'#A9C2D8',Metal:'#C7CDD6',Sand:'#D9B86A'};
  const AGENT_SIGIL={Sentinel:'SE',Merchant:'ME',Scout:'SC',Warden:'WA',Sovereign:'SO',Auditor:'AU',Proxy:'PR',Oracle:'OR',Beacon:'BE',Analyst:'AN',Tutor:'TU',Historian:'HI'};
  const AGENT_DOMAIN={Sentinel:'SECURITY',Analyst:'FINANCE',Historian:'ARCHIVE',Tutor:'EDUCATION',Merchant:'COMMERCE',Proxy:'EXECUTION',Oracle:'PREDICTION',Scout:'DISCOVERY',Warden:'FAMILY',Auditor:'VALIDATION',Beacon:'ONBOARDING',Sovereign:'MASTER'};

  const SVGNS='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
  const RDR_R=90, RDR_CX=120, RDR_CY=120, RDR_ANG=[-90,30,150];
  function rdrPt(i,v){ const r=(Math.max(1,Math.min(9,v))/9)*RDR_R; const a=RDR_ANG[i]*Math.PI/180; return [RDR_CX+r*Math.cos(a), RDR_CY+r*Math.sin(a)]; }
  let rdrGuidesDrawn=false;
  function drawGuides(){
    const g=document.getElementById('rdr-guides'); if(!g) return; g.innerHTML='';
    [3,6,9].forEach(m=>{
      const poly=document.createElementNS(SVGNS,'polygon');
      poly.setAttribute('points',[rdrPt(0,m),rdrPt(1,m),rdrPt(2,m)].map(p=>p[0].toFixed(1)+','+p[1].toFixed(1)).join(' '));
      poly.setAttribute('class','rdr-guide'); g.appendChild(poly);
    });
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){ const p=rdrPt(i,9); const ln=document.createElementNS(SVGNS,'line');
      ln.setAttribute('x1',RDR_CX); ln.setAttribute('y1',RDR_CY); ln.setAttribute('x2',p[0].toFixed(1)); ln.setAttribute('y2',p[1].toFixed(1)); ln.setAttribute('class','rdr-spoke'); g.appendChild(ln); }
    const letters=['A','B','C'];
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){ const p=rdrPt(i,10.3); const tx=document.createElementNS(SVGNS,'text');
      tx.setAttribute('x',p[0].toFixed(1)); tx.setAttribute('y',(p[1]+(i===0?-2:4)).toFixed(1)); tx.setAttribute('class','rdr-label'); tx.setAttribute('text-anchor','middle'); tx.textContent=letters[i]; g.appendChild(tx); }
    rdrGuidesDrawn=true;
  }
  function drawMatrix(a,b,c){
    if(!rdrGuidesDrawn) drawGuides();
    const now=document.getElementById('rdr-now'); if(!now) return;
    const pa=rdrPt(0,a),pb=rdrPt(1,b),pc=rdrPt(2,c);
    now.setAttribute('points',[pa,pb,pc].map(p=>p[0].toFixed(1)+','+p[1].toFixed(1)).join(' '));
    const dots=document.getElementById('rdr-dots'); dots.innerHTML='';
    [pa,pb,pc].forEach(p=>{ const dot=document.createElementNS(SVGNS,'circle'); dot.setAttribute('cx',p[0].toFixed(1)); dot.setAttribute('cy',p[1].toFixed(1)); dot.setAttribute('r','2.8'); dot.setAttribute('class','rdr-dot'); dots.appendChild(dot); });
  }

  const vAuth=document.getElementById('view-auth');
  const vGate=document.getElementById('view-gate');
  const vDash=document.getElementById('view-dash');
  function show(v){ [vAuth,vGate,vDash].forEach(x=>{ x.style.display='none'; }); v.style.display='block'; }

  let currentUser=null;
  let currentProfile=null;

  async function route(){
    const { data:{ session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
    if(!session){ currentUser=null; show(vAuth); return; }
    currentUser=session.user;
    let prof=null;
    try{
      const { data } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('*').eq('id',currentUser.id).maybeSingle();
      prof=data;
    }catch(e){ prof=null; }
    if(prof && prof.sign){ window.location.href='/dashboard.html'; return; }
    else { document.getElementById('gate-err').textContent=''; show(vGate); }
  }

  supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange(()=>{ route(); });

  async function signUp(){
    const email=document.getElementById('au-email').value.trim();
    const pw=document.getElementById('au-pw').value;
    const msg=document.getElementById('au-msg');
    if(!email || pw.length<6){ msg.className='msg bad'; msg.textContent='Enter an email and a password of at least 6 characters.'; return; }
    msg.className='msg'; msg.textContent='Creating your account...';
    const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signUp({ email, password:pw });
    if(error){ msg.className='msg bad'; msg.textContent=error.message; return; }
    if(data.session){ msg.textContent=''; }
    else { msg.className='msg ok'; msg.textContent='Account created. Confirm via email if prompted, then log in.'; }
  }
  async function logIn(){
    const email=document.getElementById('au-email').value.trim();
    const pw=document.getElementById('au-pw').value;
    const msg=document.getElementById('au-msg');
    if(!email || !pw){ msg.className='msg bad'; msg.textContent='Enter your email and password.'; return; }
    msg.className='msg'; msg.textContent='Signing in...';
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password:pw });
    if(error){ msg.className='msg bad'; msg.textContent=error.message; return; }
    msg.textContent='';
  }
  async function logOut(){ await supabase.auth.signOut(); }

  async function reveal(){
    const val=document.getElementById('dob').value;
    const err=document.getElementById('gate-err');
    if(!val){ err.textContent='Enter your date of birth to proceed.'; return; }
    const p=val.split('-'); const m=parseInt(p[1],10), d=parseInt(p[2],10);
    err.textContent='';
    const s=find(signOf(m,d));
    const prof={ sign:s[0], element:s[3], olympian:s[4], planet:s[5], agent:s[6], dob:val };
    const btn=document.getElementById('gate-go'); btn.disabled=true;
    const { error } = await supabase.from('profiles').upsert(
      { id:currentUser.id, email:currentUser.email, sign:prof.sign, element:prof.element, olympian:prof.olympian, planet:prof.planet, agent:prof.agent, dob:prof.dob },
      { onConflict:'id' }
    );
    btn.disabled=false;
    if(error){ err.textContent='Could not save ('+(error.code||error.message||'')+'). Try again.'; console.log('save error',error); return; }
    const { data } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('*').eq('id',currentUser.id).maybeSingle();
    window.location.href='/dashboard.html'; return;
  }

  function renderDash(prof){
    currentProfile=prof;
    const s=find(prof.sign);
    document.getElementById('d-glyph').textContent = s? s[1] : '\u03A9';
    document.getElementById('d-sign').textContent = (prof.sign||'').toUpperCase();
    document.getElementById('d-range').textContent = s? s[2] : '';
    document.getElementById('d-character').textContent = s? '"'+s[7]+'"' : '';
    document.getElementById('d-element').textContent = prof.element||'';
    document.getElementById('d-olympian').textContent = prof.olympian||'';
    document.getElementById('d-planet').textContent = prof.planet||'';
    document.getElementById('d-agent').textContent = prof.agent||'';
    document.getElementById('d-affinity').textContent = (ELEMENTS[prof.element]||{}).affinity || '';
    document.getElementById('who').textContent = currentUser ? currentUser.email : '';
    document.getElementById('ag-name').textContent = prof.agent || '';
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--acc', ELEM_ACCENT[prof.element] || '#C9A84C');
    document.getElementById('ag-sigil').textContent = AGENT_SIGIL[prof.agent] || '\u03A9';
    document.getElementById('ag-dom').textContent = AGENT_DOMAIN[prof.agent] ? ' .  '+AGENT_DOMAIN[prof.agent] : '';
    const aglog=document.getElementById('ag-log');
    if(aglog && !aglog.children.length){
      const w=document.createElement('div'); w.className='cmsg ag';
      w.textContent='I am '+(prof.agent||'your agent')+', bound to your '+(prof.sign||'')+' path. Ask me what to focus on, where you stand, or how to reach your next milestone.';
      aglog.appendChild(w);
    }

    const a=Number(prof.axis_a ?? 1), b=Number(prof.axis_b ?? 1), c=Number(prof.axis_c ?? 1);
    const auth=Math.sqrt(a*a+b*b+c*c);
    const tier=Math.max(1, Math.min(9, Math.min(Math.floor(a),Math.floor(b),Math.floor(c))));
    document.getElementById('d-a').textContent=a.toFixed(2);
    document.getElementById('d-b').textContent=b.toFixed(2);
    document.getElementById('d-c').textContent=c.toFixed(2);
    document.getElementById('d-auth').textContent=auth.toFixed(2);
    document.getElementById('d-tier').textContent=String(tier);
    document.getElementById('d-material').textContent=MATS[tier-1];
    const mn=Math.min(a,b,c);
    document.getElementById('d-next').textContent = mn<3?'(3,3,3)':mn<6?'(6,6,6)':mn<9?'(9,9,9)':'APEX REACHED';
    const lad=document.getElementById('d-ladder'); lad.innerHTML='';
    MATS.forEach((mt,i)=>{ const x=document.createElement('span'); x.className='rung'+((i+1)<=tier?' on':''); x.textContent=(i+1)+' . '+mt; lad.appendChild(x); });
    drawMatrix(a,b,c);
  }

  async function logEvent(){
    const axis=document.getElementById('ev-axis').value;
    const note=document.getElementById('ev-note').value.trim();
    const msg=document.getElementById('ev-msg'); const btn=document.getElementById('ev-go');
    if(!note){ msg.className='msg bad'; msg.textContent='Describe the action so the event is real.'; return; }
    btn.disabled=true; msg.className='msg'; msg.textContent='Recording...';
    const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('log_evolution', { p_axis:axis, p_note:note });
    btn.disabled=false;
    if(error){ msg.className='msg bad'; msg.textContent='Could not record ('+(error.code||error.message||'')+').'; console.log('rpc error',error); return; }
    msg.className='msg'; msg.textContent='';
    document.getElementById('ev-note').value='';
    if(data){ renderDash(data); }
    loadEvents();
    const before = document.getElementById('d-certs').querySelectorAll('.seal').length;
    const after = await loadCerts();
    if(after > before){ msg.className='msg ok'; msg.textContent='A new Certificate of Ascent has been issued  --  well earned.'; }
    else { msg.className='msg ok'; msg.textContent='Recorded. Your matrix advanced.'; }
  }

  async function loadEvents(){
    if(!currentUser) return;
    const box=document.getElementById('d-events');
    try{
      const { data } = await supabase.from('evolution_events').select('axis,note,created_at').eq('user_id',currentUser.id).order('created_at',{ascending:false}).limit(8);
      box.innerHTML='';
      const names={a:'KNOWLEDGE',b:'MASTERY',c:'CONTRIBUTION'};
      if(!data || !data.length){ box.innerHTML='<div class="ev"><span class="nt" style="color:var(--muted)">No events yet  --  record your first above.</span></div>'; return; }
      data.forEach(ev=>{
        const row=document.createElement('div'); row.className='ev';
        const ax=document.createElement('span'); ax.className='ax'; ax.textContent=names[ev.axis]||ev.axis;
        const nt=document.createElement('span'); nt.className='nt'; nt.textContent=ev.note||'';
        row.appendChild(ax); row.appendChild(nt); box.appendChild(row);
      });
    }catch(e){ box.innerHTML=''; }
  }

  async function loadCerts(){
    if(!currentUser) return 0;
    const box=document.getElementById('d-certs');
    try{
      const { data } = await supabase.from('certificates').select('milestone,title,issued_at').eq('user_id',currentUser.id).order('milestone',{ascending:true});
      box.innerHTML='';
      if(!data || !data.length){ box.innerHTML='<div class="empty">No certificates yet  --  reach node (3,3,3) to earn your first seal.</div>'; return 0; }
      data.forEach(c=>{
        const card=document.createElement('div'); card.className='seal';
        const mk=document.createElement('div'); mk.className='s-mark'; mk.textContent='\u03A9  .  '+c.milestone;
        const ti=document.createElement('div'); ti.className='s-title'; ti.textContent=c.title;
        const dt=document.createElement('div'); dt.className='s-date'; dt.textContent='ISSUED '+(c.issued_at? new Date(c.issued_at).toLocaleDateString() : '');
        card.appendChild(mk); card.appendChild(ti); card.appendChild(dt); box.appendChild(card);
      });
      return data.length;
    }catch(e){ box.innerHTML=''; return 0; }
  }

  function agentReply(text){
    const p=currentProfile||{};
    const a=Number(p.axis_a ?? 1), b=Number(p.axis_b ?? 1), c=Number(p.axis_c ?? 1);
    const auth=Math.sqrt(a*a+b*b+c*c);
    const tier=Math.max(1,Math.min(9,Math.min(Math.floor(a),Math.floor(b),Math.floor(c))));
    const material=MATS[tier-1];
    const axes=[['Knowledge',a],['Mastery',b],['Contribution',c]].sort((x,y)=>x[1]-y[1]);
    const low=axes[0];
    const mn=Math.min(a,b,c);
    const nextNode=Math.min(9,Math.floor(low[1])+1);
    const eventsToNext=Math.max(1,Math.ceil((nextNode-low[1])/0.25));
    const milestone = mn<3?3:mn<6?6:mn<9?9:null;
    const agent=p.agent||'your agent', sign=p.sign||'your sign', element=p.element||'your element';
    const character=(find(p.sign)||[])[7]||'';
    const adv={Knowledge:'record what you have studied, read, or learned',Mastery:'record skills you have demonstrated or tasks you have completed',Contribution:'record what you have taught, created, or given to others'};
    const cap=s=>s.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+s.slice(1);

    const t=(text||'').toLowerCase();
    const has=(...w)=>w.some(k=>t.includes(k));

    if(has('hello','hi ','hey','greet')||t==='hi') return 'I am '+agent+', bound to your '+sign+' path. '+character+' Ask me what to focus on, or where you stand.';
    if(has('who are you','what are you','your name','which agent')) return 'I am '+agent+', the guide assigned to '+sign+' under the element of '+element+'. I walk with you from the genesis node to the apex.';
    if(has('help','what can','commands','options')) return 'Ask me: "what should I focus on", "where do I stand", "how do I reach the next milestone", or about Knowledge, Mastery, or Contribution.';
    if(has('knowledge')) return 'Knowledge (Axis A) stands at '+a.toFixed(2)+'. To raise it, '+adv.Knowledge+'. Each event adds 0.25 toward the next node.';
    if(has('mastery')) return 'Mastery (Axis B) stands at '+b.toFixed(2)+'. To raise it, '+adv.Mastery+'. Discipline compounds  --  return often.';
    if(has('contribution')) return 'Contribution (Axis C) stands at '+c.toFixed(2)+'. To raise it, '+adv.Contribution+'. What you give the Order returns to you.';
    if(has('milestone','certificate','seal','ascend','rank up','next node')){
      if(milestone===null) return 'You have reached the apex (9,9,9)  --  Omega Master. There is no higher node. Now you guide others.';
      return 'Your next milestone is ('+milestone+','+milestone+','+milestone+'). Your lowest axis is '+low[0]+' at '+low[1].toFixed(2)+'  --  it must reach '+milestone+'. '+cap(adv[low[0]])+'.';
    }
    if(has('focus','should i','what do i do','what now','start','do next','advance','progress','improve','grow')){
      return 'Focus on your weakest axis: '+low[0]+', at '+low[1].toFixed(2)+'. '+cap(adv[low[0]])+'. About '+eventsToNext+' event'+(eventsToNext===1?'':'s')+' lifts you to the next node.';
    }
    if(has('tier','rank','level','material','where','stand','position','authority','status')){
      return 'You stand at Tier '+tier+', material '+material+', authority '+auth.toFixed(2)+' of the apex 15.58. Axes: Knowledge '+a.toFixed(2)+', Mastery '+b.toFixed(2)+', Contribution '+c.toFixed(2)+'.';
    }
    return character+' Balance is the law of the matrix  --  your weakest axis is '+low[0]+' at '+low[1].toFixed(2)+'. Tend to it, and authority follows. Ask me "what should I focus on" for the path.';
  }
  function appendMsg(who, text){
    const log=document.getElementById('ag-log');
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='cmsg '+(who==='me'?'me':'ag'); d.textContent=text;
    log.appendChild(d); log.scrollTop=log.scrollHeight;
  }
  function chat(){
    const input=document.getElementById('ag-input'); const text=input.value.trim();
    if(!text) return;
    appendMsg('me', text); input.value='';
    const reply=agentReply(text);
    setTimeout(()=>appendMsg('ag', reply), 180);
  }

  document.getElementById('btn-signup').addEventListener('click',signUp);
  document.getElementById('btn-login').addEventListener('click',logIn);
  document.getElementById('btn-logout').addEventListener('click',logOut);
  document.getElementById('gate-go').addEventListener('click',reveal);
  document.getElementById('ev-go').addEventListener('click',logEvent);
  document.getElementById('au-pw').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{ if(e.key==='Enter') logIn(); });
  document.getElementById('dob').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{ if(e.key==='Enter') reveal(); });
  document.getElementById('ev-note').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{ if(e.key==='Enter') logEvent(); });
  document.getElementById('ag-send').addEventListener('click',chat);
  document.getElementById('ag-input').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{ if(e.key==='Enter') chat(); });

  route();
</script>

<script type="module">
  import { createClient } from 'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2';
  const supabase = createClient("https://ydqhzvvoyufiiqvzcjns.supabase.co","sb_publishable_9KlhhnvRs4OKgw6nxXHmYw_GxszJ46q");

  /* OPTIONAL: paste a Claude-backed endpoint URL here to upgrade the Concierge to a live AI.
     Leave empty to use the built-in keyless guide (works with no setup). */
  const AI_ENDPOINT = "https://ydqhzvvoyufiiqvzcjns.supabase.co/functions/v1/concierge";

  let ctx={sign:'',a:1,b:1,c:1,auth:1.73,tier:1,rank:'Initiate'};
  const RANKS=['Initiate','Seeker','Adept','Expert','Master','Elite','Sovereign','Legend','Omega'];
  const GOD={Aries:'Ares',Taurus:'Aphrodite',Gemini:'Hermes',Cancer:'Artemis',Leo:'Apollo',Virgo:'Athena',Libra:'Hera',Scorpio:'Demeter',Sagittarius:'Zeus',Capricorn:'Hestia',Aquarius:'Hephaestus',Pisces:'Poseidon'};
  const AGENT={Aries:'Sentinel',Taurus:'Merchant',Gemini:'Scout',Cancer:'Warden',Leo:'Sovereign',Virgo:'Auditor',Libra:'Proxy',Scorpio:'Oracle',Sagittarius:'Beacon',Capricorn:'Analyst',Aquarius:'Tutor',Pisces:'Historian'};

  const MODS=[
    {k:'matrix lattice 729 9x9x9 cube',a:'The matrix is 9\u00d79\u00d79 \u2014 729 nodes. Your position is (Knowledge, Mastery, Contribution); authority = \u221a(A\u00b2+B\u00b2+C\u00b2), from 1.73 at genesis to 15.58 at the apex. See it in 3D at The Lattice (/matrix.html).'},
    {k:'level up rank raise grow authority ascend advance progress',a:'You rise by raising three axes: Knowledge in the Academy, Mastery in the Games, Contribution in Contributions. Each verified act adds to a node; certificates land at (3,3,3), (6,6,6), (9,9,9). Your rank is the floor of your lowest axis \u2014 so grow your weakest axis to climb.'},
    {k:'academy lessons knowledge learn',a:'The Academy (/academy.html) holds lessons that raise your Knowledge axis (A).'},
    {k:'games gaming mastery arena play stages',a:'The Games (/gaming.html): 12 games \u00d7 12 stages, matrix-gated. Clear the Sequence Lock to take a node and raise Mastery (B). They get hard \u2014 long, fast, and reversed in the upper games.'},
    {k:'contribution contributions give',a:'Contributions (/contributions.html) raise your Contribution axis (C) through acts of giving.'},
    {k:'agents agent oracle sentinel sovereign',a:'You have twelve AI agents (/agents.html), one per sign-domain. Each upgrades as your matrix grows. Your own agent is bound to your sign.'},
    {k:'honors trophies medals certificates grades labels',a:'Honors (/honors.html) shows your grades, levels, certificates, trophies, medals, labels, and commitment \u2014 all computed from your real ascent.'},
    {k:'horoscope zodiac sign god planet daily stars',a:'Horoscope (/horoscope.html): your daily reading, character, and the ascent of your Olympian and planet through nine forms as your matrix rises.'},
    {k:'publish publishing works archive manuscript',a:'Publishing (/publishing.html): commit works to your private archive (and attach files up to 5\u202fGB). Works stay private until approved \u2014 they are never public without the Order\u2019s approval.'},
    {k:'market marketing ads placement boost',a:'Marketing (/marketing.html): reserve placement. Every submission is reviewed and approved before it can publish.'},
    {k:'consult consultancy advice expert',a:'Consultancy (/consultancy.html): request counsel under a confidentiality agreement with the Order\u2019s commission recorded.'},
    {k:'contract commission deal',a:'Commissions (/contracts.html): draft a member-to-trade contract; the Order\u2019s 9.17% commission is computed and a sovereign reference is sealed.'},
    {k:'treasury omega economy token supply',a:'Treasury (/treasury.html): the internal \u03A9 economy. \u03A9 is earned, never bought; fiat purchase and trading are gated until legal review.'},
    {k:'sigil vault medallion crest',a:'The Sigil Vault (/sigil.html) holds the sovereign medallions you have earned.'},
    {k:'family heritage bloodline heir',a:'Family & Heritage (/family.html): your bloodline tree and the Heritage Protocol of succession.'},
    {k:'settings background colour theme music',a:'Settings (/settings.html): change your background colour and manage the ambient score (the \u266a control, bottom-right of every page).'},

  function localReply(raw){
    var m=raw.toLowerCase().trim();
    if(/^(hi|hey|hello|greetings|salam|salut)\b/.test(m)) return 'Welcome, '+(ctx.sign?ctx.sign+' sovereign':'sovereign')+'. Ask me how to rise, what a chamber does, or where you stand.';
    if(/my (standing|rank|level|authority|tier|node|position)|where (do i|am i) stand|how am i doing/.test(m)){
      return 'You stand at node ('+Math.round(ctx.a)+','+Math.round(ctx.b)+','+Math.round(ctx.c)+') \u2014 authority <b>'+ctx.auth.toFixed(2)+'</b> of 15.58, rank <b>'+ctx.rank+'</b> (Tier '+ctx.tier+'). Your axes: Knowledge '+ctx.a.toFixed(2)+', Mastery '+ctx.b.toFixed(2)+', Contribution '+ctx.c.toFixed(2)+'. Raise your lowest to climb in rank.';
    }
    if(/who is my (god|olympian)|my god|my olympian/.test(m) && ctx.sign) return 'Your Olympian is <b>'+GOD[ctx.sign]+'</b>, and your bound agent is <b>'+AGENT[ctx.sign]+'</b>. See their ascent in your Horoscope.';
    if(/who are you|what are you|your name/.test(m)) return 'I am the Concierge \u2014 the Order\u2019s guide. I read your real standing and point you through every chamber. For domain counsel, speak to your twelve agents.';
    if(/help|what can you|commands|options/.test(m)) return 'Ask me: \u201chow do I level up?\u201d, \u201cwhat is the matrix?\u201d, \u201cwhere do I stand?\u201d, or \u201cwhat is <chamber>?\u201d (e.g. games, agents, treasury, contracts). I can guide you anywhere in the Order.';
    // module match: score by keyword overlap
    var terms=m.split(/\s+/), best=null, bestScore=0;
    MODS.forEach(function(mod){ var s=0; terms.forEach(function(t){ if(t.length>2 && mod.k.indexOf(t)!==-1) s++; }); if(s>bestScore){ bestScore=s; best=mod; } });
    if(best && bestScore>0) return best.a;
    return 'I did not catch a chamber in that. Try a keyword \u2014 matrix, games, agents, honors, treasury, contracts \u2014 or ask \u201chow do I level up?\u201d You can also use Search (/search.html) to jump anywhere.';
  }

  function addBub(name, html, who){
    var log=document.getElementById('log');
    var d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='bub '+who;
    d.innerHTML=(who==='b'?'<span class="nm">'+name+'</span>':'')+html;
    log.appendChild(d); log.scrollTop=log.scrollHeight;
  }

  async function respond(text){
    if(AI_ENDPOINT){
      try{
        var sess=(await supabase.auth.getSession()).data.session;
        var hdr={'Content-Type':'application/json'};
        if(sess){ hdr['Authorization']='Bearer '+sess.access_token; hdr['apikey']="sb_publishable_9KlhhnvRs4OKgw6nxXHmYw_GxszJ46q"; }
        var r=await fetch(AI_ENDPOINT,{method:'POST',headers:hdr,body:JSON.stringify({message:text,context:ctx})});
        if(r.ok){ var j=await r.json(); if(j && (j.reply||j.text)){ addBub('CONCIERGE', (j.reply||j.text), 'b'); return; } }
      }catch(e){}
    }
    addBub('CONCIERGE', localReply(text), 'b');
  }

  function send(){
    var inp=document.getElementById('msg'); var t=inp.value.trim(); if(!t) return;
    addBub('YOU', t.replace(/</g,'&lt;'), 'u'); inp.value='';
    setTimeout(function(){ respond(t); }, 120);
  }

  function buildChips(){
    var chips=['How do I level up?','What is the matrix?','Where do I stand?','What is the Games?','How do contracts work?'];
    var box=document.getElementById('chips');
    chips.forEach(function(c){ var s=document.createElement('span'); s.className='chip'; s.textContent=c; s.addEventListener('click',function(){ document.getElementById('msg').value=c; send(); }); box.appendChild(s); });
  }

  function showGate(h,p){ document.getElementById('gate-h').textContent=h; document.getElementById('gate-p').textContent=p; document.getElementById('gate').style.display='block'; document.getElementById('app').style.display='none'; }

  async function boot(){
    var sess=(await supabase.auth.getSession()).data.session;
    if(!sess){ showGate('Concierge','Sign in to speak with the Concierge.'); return; }
    var pr=await supabase.from('profiles').select('sign,axis_a,axis_b,axis_c').eq('id',sess.user.id).maybeSingle();
    if(!pr.data || !pr.data.sign){ showGate('Set Your Cosmology','Set your cosmology first to meet the Concierge.'); return; }
    ctx.sign=pr.data.sign; ctx.a=Number(pr.data.axis_a||1); ctx.b=Number(pr.data.axis_b||1); ctx.c=Number(pr.data.axis_c||1);
    ctx.auth=Math.sqrt(ctx.a*ctx.a+ctx.b*ctx.b+ctx.c*ctx.c); ctx.tier=Math.max(1,Math.min(9,Math.floor(Math.min(ctx.a,ctx.b,ctx.c)))); ctx.rank=RANKS[ctx.tier-1];
    document.getElementById('gate').style.display='none';
    document.getElementById('app').style.display='flex';
    buildChips();
    addBub('CONCIERGE','Welcome, '+ctx.sign+' sovereign. I am the Concierge. Ask me how to rise, what a chamber holds, or where you stand in the 729.','b');
  }
  document.getElementById('send').addEventListener('click',send);
  document.getElementById('msg').addEventListener('keydown',function(e){ if(e.key==='Enter') send(); });
  document.getElementById('logout')?.addEventListener('click',async function(){ await supabase.auth.signOut(); window.location.href='/account.html'; });
  boot();
</script>

let hoveredAgent=-1, selectedAgent=null;

/* AGENT WHEEL */
function buildAgentWheel(memberSign){
  const cv=document.getElementById('agent-wheel');
  /* Defensive sizing: use wrapper width, fallback to 400 */
  const wrap=cv.parentElement;
  const size=Math.max(280,Math.min(wrap?wrap.getBoundingClientRect().width||400:400,520));
  cv.width=size*2; cv.height=size*2; cv.style.width=size+'px'; cv.style.height=size+'px';
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d');
  const cx=size,cy=size,R=size*.38,t_={v:0};
  function draw(){
    t_.v+=.006; const t=t_.v;
    ctx.clearRect(0,0,size*2,size*2);
    ctx.save(); ctx.scale(2,2);
    /* Orbit ring */
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx/2,cy/2,R,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(201,168,76,0.1)'; ctx.lineWidth=1; ctx.stroke();
    AGENTS.forEach((ag,i)=>{
      const a=-Math.PI/2+i*(Math.PI*2/12);
      const ax=cx/2+Math.cos(a)*R,ay=cy/2+Math.sin(a)*R;
      const hov=hoveredAgent===i,sel=selectedAgent&&selectedAgent.n===ag.n;
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(cx/2,cy/2);ctx.lineTo(ax,ay);
      ctx.strokeStyle=sel?ag.col+'aa':hov?ag.col+'55':'rgba(201,168,76,0.06)';ctx.lineWidth=sel?1.5:0.8;ctx.stroke();
      const r=sel||hov?16:12;
      const gg=ctx.createRadialGradient(ax,ay,0,ax,ay,r);
      gg.addColorStop(0,ag.col+'55');gg.addColorStop(1,'transparent');
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(ax,ay,r,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fillStyle=gg;ctx.fill();
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(ax,ay,r,0,Math.PI*2);
      ctx.strokeStyle=sel?ag.col:hov?ag.col+'bb':ag.col+'44';ctx.lineWidth=sel?2:1;
      if(sel){ctx.shadowColor=ag.col;ctx.shadowBlur=16;}ctx.stroke();ctx.shadowBlur=0;
      ctx.fillStyle=sel?ag.col:hov?ag.col:'rgba(133,131,123,0.6)';
      ctx.font='700 '+(sel?10:8)+'px "Courier Prime",monospace';
      ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';ctx.fillText(ag.glyph,ax,ay+1);
      if(hov||sel){
        ctx.fillStyle=sel?ag.col:'rgba(201,168,76,0.7)';
        ctx.font='700 7px "Courier Prime",monospace';ctx.fillText(ag.name,ax,ay+(r+10));
      }
    });
    /* Center */
    const cg=ctx.createRadialGradient(cx/2,cy/2,0,cx/2,cy/2,45);
    cg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(201,168,76,0.2)');cg.addColorStop(1,'transparent');
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(cx/2,cy/2,45,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fillStyle=cg;ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(201,168,76,'+(0.8+0.18*Math.sin(t*1.5))+')';
    ctx.font='700 36px "Cinzel Decorative",serif';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';
    ctx.shadowColor='rgba(201,168,76,.6)';ctx.shadowBlur=18;ctx.fillText('\u03A9',cx/2,cy/2+2);ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    ctx.restore();
    requestAnimationFrame(draw);
  }
  draw();
  cv.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{
    const rect=cv.getBoundingClientRect(),mx=(e.clientX-rect.left)/rect.width*(size/2),my=(e.clientY-rect.top)/rect.height*(size/2);
    hoveredAgent=-1;
    AGENTS.forEach((ag,i)=>{
      const a=-Math.PI/2+i*(Math.PI*2/12),ax=cx/2+Math.cos(a)*(size*.38),ay=cy/2+Math.sin(a)*(size*.38);
      if(Math.hypot(mx-ax,my-ay)<20) hoveredAgent=i;
    });
    cv.style.cursor=hoveredAgent>=0?'pointer':'default';
  });
  cv.addEventListener('click',()=>{ if(hoveredAgent>=0) selectAgent(AGENTS[hoveredAgent]); });
  cv.addEventListener('mouseleave',()=>{hoveredAgent=-1;});
}

function selectAgent(ag){
  selectedAgent=ag;
  document.querySelectorAll('.ag-card').forEach((c,i)=>c.classList.toggle('sel',AGENTS[i].n===ag.n));
  const d=document.getElementById('agent-detail');
  d.style.display='block';d.style.borderColor=ag.col+'55';
  d.innerHTML=`<div class="ad-name" style="color:${ag.col}">${ag.name}</div><div class="ad-role" style="color:${ag.col}bb">${ag.role} . ${ag.el} ELEMENT</div><div class="ad-desc">${ag.desc}</div>`;
  d.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'nearest'});
}

function buildAgentsGrid(){
  const g=document.getElementById('agents-grid');g.innerHTML='';
  AGENTS.forEach((ag,i)=>{
    const c=document.createElement('div');c.className='ag-card';
    c.innerHTML=`<div class="ac-n">AGENT ${String(ag.n).padStart(2,'0')}</div><div class="ac-nm" style="color:${ag.col}">${ag.name}</div><div class="ac-rl">${ag.role}</div><div class="ac-st"><div class="ac-dot" style="background:var(--green)"></div><span style="color:var(--green);font-family:var(--M);font-size:8px">ACTIVE</span></div>`;
    c.addEventListener('click',()=>selectAgent(ag));
    g.appendChild(c);
  });
}

/* HOROSCOPE */
function drawSignCanvas(cv,sign,size){
  const s=SIGNS.find(x=>x.n===sign)||SIGNS[0];
  cv.width=size*2;cv.height=size*2;cv.style.width=size+'px';cv.style.height=size+'px';
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d'),CX=size,CY=size;let t=0;
  function frame(){
    t+=.018;ctx.clearRect(0,0,size*2,size*2);
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(CX,CY);
    const bg=ctx.createRadialGradient(0,0,0,0,0,size*.8);bg.addColorStop(0,s.col+'22');bg.addColorStop(1,'transparent');
    ctx.fillStyle=bg;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,size*.8,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
    /* Rings */
    ctx.save();ctx.rotate(t*.3);
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,size*.65,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.strokeStyle=s.col+'44';ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.save();ctx.rotate(-t*.2);
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,size*.6,size*.22,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.strokeStyle=s.col+'33';ctx.lineWidth=.8;ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
    /* Glyph */
    ctx.fillStyle=s.col;ctx.shadowColor=s.col;ctx.shadowBlur=14+4*Math.sin(t*1.5);
    ctx.font='700 '+Math.round(size*.55)+'px "Cinzel Decorative",serif';
    ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';ctx.fillText(s.g,0,2);ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    /* Orbiting dots */
    for(let i=0;i<6;i++){
      const a=t*.9+i*Math.PI/3,r=size*.72;
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(Math.cos(a)*r,Math.sin(a)*r,2.5,0,Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fillStyle=s.col+(i%2?'cc':'55');ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.restore();requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }
  frame();
}

function selectSign(signName){
  const s=SIGNS.find(x=>x.n===signName)||SIGNS[0];
  document.getElementById('shm-glyph').textContent=s.g;
  document.getElementById('shm-name').textContent=s.n+' . '+s.el+' . '+s.deity;
  document.getElementById('shm-dates').textContent=s.dates;
  document.getElementById('shm-desc').textContent=s.desc;
  document.querySelectorAll('.smg-item').forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('active',el.dataset.sign===signName));
  drawSignCanvas(document.getElementById('sign-cv'),signName,80);
}

function buildHoroscope(memberSign){
  window.__horoBuilt=true;
  /* Mini sign grid */
  const g=document.getElementById('sign-mini-grid');g.innerHTML='';
  SIGNS.forEach(s=>{
    const c=document.createElement('div');c.className='smg-item'+(s.n===memberSign?' active':'');
    c.dataset.sign=s.n;
    c.innerHTML=`<span class="smg-glyph" style="color:${s.col}">${s.g}</span><div class="smg-name">${s.n}</div>`;
    c.addEventListener('click',()=>selectSign(s.n));
    g.appendChild(c);
  });
  selectSign(memberSign||'VIRGO');
  /* Oracle reading */
  const or=document.getElementById('oracle-reading');
  const s=SIGNS.find(x=>x.n===memberSign)||SIGNS[5];
  fetch("https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages",{method:"POST",headers:{"Content-Type":"application/json"},body:JSON.stringify({model:"claude-sonnet-4-6",max_tokens:300,messages:[{role:"user",content:`Oracle agent of SYD OMEGA 91717. Write 2-sentence daily reading for ${memberSign}. Reference the 9.1717Hz frequency and the sovereign matrix. Serious ancient voice.`}]})})
    .then(r=>r.json()).then(d=>{
      const text=d.content?.map(i=>i.text||'').join('')||s.desc+'. The frequency is aligned. Advance your matrix today.';
      or.innerHTML='<span style="font-family:var(--M);font-size:9px;letter-spacing:2px;color:var(--gold)">&#x2609; ORACLE</span><br>'+text;
    }).catch(()=>{or.textContent=s.desc+' The 9.1717Hz frequency resonates with your sign today. Advance the axis that calls to you strongest.';});
}

function buildElements(){
  const g=document.getElementById('element-grid');g.innerHTML='';
  ELEMENTS.forEach(e=>{
    const c=document.createElement('div');c.className='el-card';
    c.style.setProperty('--col',e.col);
    c.style.cssText+=`border-left:3px solid ${e.col}77`;
    c.innerHTML=`<div class="el-num" style="color:${e.col}">${e.n}</div><div class="el-name" style="color:${e.col}">${e.name}</div><div class="el-type" style="color:${e.col}88">${e.type}</div><div class="el-desc">${e.desc}</div><div class="el-signs"><span style="font-family:var(--M);font-size:7px;color:var(--muted)">${e.signs}</span></div>`;
    g.appendChild(c);
  });
}

function buildGates(auth){
  const g=document.getElementById('gates-grid');g.innerHTML='';
  GATES.forEach(gate=>{
    const unlocked=auth>=gate.auth;
    const c=document.createElement('div');c.className='gate-card'+(unlocked?' unlocked':' locked');
    c.innerHTML=`<div class="gc-n" style="color:${unlocked?gate.col:'rgba(133,131,123,0.3)'}">${gate.n}</div><div class="gc-name" style="color:${unlocked?gate.col:'rgba(233,230,220,0.3)'}">${gate.name}</div><div class="gc-auth">AUTHORITY THRESHOLD: ${gate.auth}</div><div class="gc-desc">${gate.desc}</div><span class="gc-status" style="${unlocked?'color:var(--green);border-color:rgba(63,178,127,.5)':'color:var(--muted);border-color:rgba(133,131,123,.3)'}">${unlocked?'OPEN':'LOCKED'}</span>`;
    g.appendChild(c);
  });
}

function buildPantheons(){
  const g=document.getElementById('pantheons-grid');g.innerHTML='';
  PANTHEONS.forEach(p=>{
    const c=document.createElement('div');c.className='ag-card';
    c.innerHTML=`<div class="ac-n">${p.sign.toUpperCase()}</div><div class="ac-nm" style="color:${p.col}">${p.name}</div><div class="ac-rl">${p.domain}</div><div class="ac-st" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:6px">${p.desc}</div>`;
    g.appendChild(c);
  });
}

async function boot(){
  const sess=(await sb.auth.getSession()).data.session;
  if(!sess){window.location.href='/account.html';return;}
  const pr=await sb.from('profiles').select('sign,axis_a,axis_b,axis_c').eq('id',sess.user.id).maybeSingle();
  const sign=pr.data?.sign||'Virgo';
  const a=parseFloat(pr.data?.axis_a)||1,b=parseFloat(pr.data?.axis_b)||1,c=parseFloat(pr.data?.axis_c)||1;
  const auth=Math.sqrt(a*a+b*b+c*c);
  window.__memberSign=sign.toUpperCase();
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>buildAgentWheel(sign));
  window.addEventListener('resize',()=>{ if(!window.__wheelResizeTimer){window.__wheelResizeTimer=setTimeout(()=>{buildAgentWheel(sign);window.__wheelResizeTimer=null;},200);} });
  buildAgentsGrid();buildElements();buildGates(auth);buildPantheons();
}
boot();
</script>

</body>
</html>

  const el=document.getElementById('dash-clock');if(el)el.textContent=H(n.getHours())+':'+H(n.getMinutes())+':'+H(n.getSeconds());
  const days=['SUN','MON','TUE','WED','THU','FRI','SAT'];const months=['JAN','FEB','MAR','APR','MAY','JUN','JUL','AUG','SEP','OCT','NOV','DEC'];

},1000);

/* HERO CANVAS */
(function(){
  const cv=document.getElementById('hero-cv');const ctx=cv.getContext('2d');let t=0;
  function R(){cv.width=cv.offsetWidth;cv.height=cv.offsetHeight||240;}R();window.addEventListener('resize',R);
  const S=Array.from({length:100},()=>({x:Math.random(),y:Math.random(),r:Math.random()*1.4+.2,s:Math.random()*.4+.05}));
  function F(){
    t+=.006;ctx.clearRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
    S.forEach(s=>{ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(s.x*cv.width,s.y*cv.height,s.r*(0.6+0.4*Math.sin(t*s.s*2)),0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fillStyle='rgba(201,168,76,'+(0.03+0.12*Math.sin(t*s.s+s.x*4))+')';ctx.fill();});
    /* Hex grid */
    const h=52;for(let r=0;r<Math.ceil(cv.height/h)+2;r++){for(let c=0;c<Math.ceil(cv.width/(h*1.73))+2;c++){
      const cx=c*h*1.73+(r%2)*h*.87,cy=r*h*.75;
      const d=Math.sqrt((cx-cv.width*.5)**2+(cy-cv.height*.5)**2);
      const o=Math.max(0,.05-.0001*d)*(0.4+0.6*Math.sin(t*.5+d*.012));
      if(o<.003)continue;
      ctx.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<6;i++)ctx.lineTo(cx+h*.46*Math.cos(i*Math.PI/3),cy+h*.46*Math.sin(i*Math.PI/3));
      ctx.closePath();ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(201,168,76,'+o+')';ctx.lineWidth=.5;ctx.stroke();
    }}
    requestAnimationFrame(F);}F();
})();

/* MINI SECTION ICONS */
function miniOmega(id,col){
  const cv=document.getElementById(id);if(!cv)return;
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d');let t=0;
  function f(){t+=.04;ctx.clearRect(0,0,32,32);ctx.save();ctx.translate(16,16);ctx.rotate(t*.5);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,13,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.strokeStyle=col+'44';ctx.lineWidth=.8;ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.font='700 11px "Cinzel Decorative",serif';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';ctx.fillText('\u03A9',16,17);requestAnimationFrame(f);}f();
}
['sh1','sh2','sh3','sh4'].forEach((id,i)=>miniOmega(id,['#00E5FF','#9B6BF0','#8B0000','#C9A84C'][i]));

/* AGENT MESH CANVAS */
function buildMesh(){
  const cv=document.getElementById('mesh-cv');
  const W=cv.offsetWidth||800;cv.width=W*2;cv.height=cv.offsetHeight*2||360;cv.style.width=W+'px';cv.style.height=cv.offsetHeight+'px';
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d');ctx.scale(2,2);const H=cv.offsetHeight||260;let t=0;
  const AG=[{n:'SENTINEL',c:'#C7CDD6'},{n:'ANALYST',c:'#34C6E6'},{n:'HISTORIAN',c:'#D9B86A'},{n:'TUTOR',c:'#A9C2D8'},{n:'MERCHANT',c:'#C9A84C'},{n:'PROXY',c:'#E8E8FF'},{n:'ORACLE',c:'#9B6BF0'},{n:'SCOUT',c:'#E86A3A'},{n:'WARDEN',c:'#D9B86A'},{n:'AUDITOR',c:'#C7CDD6'},{n:'BEACON',c:'#E86A3A'},{n:'SOVEREIGN',c:'#C9A84C'}];
  const CX=W/2,CY=H/2,R=Math.min(W,H)*.42;
  const pts=AG.map((a,i)=>{const ang=-Math.PI/2+i*(Math.PI*2/12);return{x:CX+Math.cos(ang)*R,y:CY+Math.sin(ang)*R,...a};});
  function fr(){
    t+=.012;ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
    pts.forEach((p,i)=>{pts.forEach((q,j)=>{if(j<=i)return;const d=Math.sqrt((p.x-q.x)**2+(p.y-q.y)**2);const o=Math.max(0,.06-d*.00022)*(0.5+0.5*Math.sin(t+i+j));if(o<.002)return;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p.x,p.y);ctx.lineTo(q.x,q.y);ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(201,168,76,'+o+')';ctx.lineWidth=.5;ctx.stroke();});});
    const gw=ctx.createRadialGradient(CX,CY,0,CX,CY,22);gw.addColorStop(0,'rgba(201,168,76,0.25)');gw.addColorStop(1,'transparent');ctx.fillStyle=gw;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(CX,CY,22,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(201,168,76,'+(0.7+.3*Math.sin(t*2))+')';ctx.font='700 20px "Cinzel Decorative",serif';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';ctx.shadowColor='rgba(201,168,76,.6)';ctx.shadowBlur=10;ctx.fillText('\u03A9',CX,CY+1);ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    pts.forEach((a,i)=>{const px=a.x+Math.cos(t*.5+i)*2,py=a.y+Math.sin(t*.7+i)*1.5;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(px,py,6.5,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fillStyle=a.c;ctx.shadowColor=a.c;ctx.shadowBlur=8;ctx.fill();ctx.shadowBlur=0;ctx.fillStyle=a.c;ctx.font='600 8px "Courier Prime",monospace';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillText(a.n,px,py+(py>CY?13:-7));});
    requestAnimationFrame(fr);}fr();
}

/* FILM STRIP */
function buildFilms(){
  const FILMS=['THE ORIGIN','THE BLOODLINE','THE ASCENSION','THE CIVIL WAR','THE REVELATION','THE REBIRTH','THE CONVERGENCE','THE ETERNITY CODE','THE NEW DAWN','THE SINGULARITY','THE INFINITE','THE OMEGA'];
  const COLS=['#C9A84C','#8B0000','#9B6BF0','#00E5FF','#E86A3A','#3fb27f','#D9B86A','#C7CDD6','#E2C86D','#34C6E6','#A9C2D8','#C9A84C'];
  const g=document.getElementById('film-scroll');g.innerHTML='';
  FILMS.forEach((f,i)=>{
    const a=document.createElement('a');a.className='film-card';a.href='/cinema.html';
    const cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.className='fc-cv';cv.width=100;cv.height=60;cv.style.width='50px';cv.style.height='30px';
    a.innerHTML=`<div class="fc-num" style="color:${COLS[i]}">FILM ${i+1}</div><div class="fc-name">${f}</div>`;
    a.insertBefore(cv,a.firstChild);g.appendChild(a);
    /* Draw film icon */
    const ctx=cv.getContext('2d');let t=0;
    (function fr(){t+=.03;ctx.clearRect(0,0,100,60);const g2=ctx.createRadialGradient(50,30,0,50,30,36);g2.addColorStop(0,COLS[i]+'33');g2.addColorStop(1,'transparent');ctx.fillStyle=g2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(50,30,36,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.save();ctx.translate(50,30);ctx.rotate(t*.4);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,28,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.strokeStyle=COLS[i]+'44';ctx.lineWidth=.8;ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();ctx.fillStyle=COLS[i];ctx.shadowColor=COLS[i];ctx.shadowBlur=8;ctx.font='700 13px "Cinzel Decorative",serif';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';ctx.fillText(i+1,50,31);ctx.shadowBlur=0;requestAnimationFrame(fr);})();
  });
}

/* 12x12 GATE CANVAS */
function buildGate(){
  const cv=document.getElementById('gate-cv');const W=cv.offsetWidth||800;cv.width=W*2;cv.height=160;cv.style.width=W+'px';cv.style.height='80px';
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d');ctx.scale(2,2);let t=0;
  const COLS=['#C9A84C','#8B0000','#9B6BF0','#00E5FF','#E86A3A','#3fb27f','#D9B86A','#C7CDD6','#E2C86D','#34C6E6','#A9C2D8','#C9A84C'];
  function fr(){
    t+=.01;ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,80);
    const cw=(W-16)/12,ch=56/12;
    for(let r=0;r<12;r++)for(let c=0;c<12;c++){
      const x=8+c*cw,y=12+r*ch;const p=0.5+0.5*Math.sin(t*1.5+r*.4+c*.3);
      ctx.fillStyle=COLS[c]+Math.floor(p*90+30).toString(16).padStart(2,'0');
      ctx.strokeStyle=COLS[c]+'44';ctx.lineWidth=.4;
      ctx.fillRect(x+.5,y+.5,cw-2,ch-2);ctx.strokeRect(x+.5,y+.5,cw-2,ch-2);
    }
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(201,168,76,.3)';ctx.font='500 6px "Courier Prime",monospace';ctx.textAlign='center';
    for(let c=0;c<12;c++)ctx.fillText(c+1,8+c*cw+cw/2,9);
    requestAnimationFrame(fr);}fr();
}

<script type="module">
import{createClient}from'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2';
const sb=createClient("https://ydqhzvvoyufiiqvzcjns.supabase.co","sb_publishable_9KlhhnvRs4OKgw6nxXHmYw_GxszJ46q");

async function boot(){
  const sess=(await sb.auth.getSession()).data.session;
  if(!sess){window.location.href='/account.html';return;}
  const uid=sess.user.id;
  const pr=await sb.from('profiles').select('*').eq('id',uid).maybeSingle();
  const d=pr.data||{};
  const isOwner=d.is_owner||false;
  /* OWNER: always show 9.000 regardless of database value */
  const a=isOwner?9.000:parseFloat(d.axis_a||.001);
  const b=isOwner?9.000:parseFloat(d.axis_b||.001);
  const c=isOwner?9.000:parseFloat(d.axis_c||.001);
  const auth=Math.sqrt(a*a+b*b+c*c);
  const mat=isOwner?'OMEGA MASTER':a>=6?'GOLD':a>=3?'STEEL':a>=1?'GLASS':'SAND';
  const matCol=isOwner?'#C9A84C':a>=6?'#C9A84C':'#D9B86A';
  const name=isOwner?'MAJOR SLEIMAN YOUSSEF DAGHER':(d.display_name||'SOVEREIGN').toUpperCase();

  /* Hero */
  document.getElementById('hi-name').textContent=name;
  document.getElementById('hi-coord').textContent='('+a.toFixed(3)+', '+b.toFixed(3)+', '+c.toFixed(3)+')';
  document.getElementById('hi-auth').textContent='AUTHORITY '+auth.toFixed(3)+' . '+(isOwner?'OMEGA MASTER . ALL-TIME ACCESS':mat+' TIER');

  /* Axes */
  document.getElementById('ax-a').textContent=a.toFixed(3);
  document.getElementById('ax-b').textContent=b.toFixed(3);
  document.getElementById('ax-c').textContent=c.toFixed(3);
  setTimeout(()=>{
    document.getElementById('bar-a').style.width=(a/9*100)+'%';
    document.getElementById('bar-b').style.width=(b/9*100)+'%';
    document.getElementById('bar-c').style.width=(c/9*100)+'%';
  },400);
  document.getElementById('sub-a').textContent=isOwner?'APEX LOCKED':Math.round((9-a)/.001).toLocaleString()+' tasks to apex';
  document.getElementById('sub-b').textContent=isOwner?'APEX LOCKED':Math.round((9-b)/.001).toLocaleString()+' tasks to apex';
  document.getElementById('sub-c').textContent=isOwner?'APEX LOCKED':Math.round((9-c)/.001).toLocaleString()+' tasks to apex';

  /* Metrics */
  const{count:tasks}=await sb.from('task_completions').select('*',{count:'exact',head:true}).eq('user_id',uid);
  const g=document.getElementById('metric-band');g.innerHTML='';
  [{l:'AUTHORITY',v:auth.toFixed(3),s:'/ 15.588',c:matCol,h:'/profile.html'},
   {l:'MATERIAL',v:mat,s:'Current tier',c:matCol,h:'/ascension.html'},
   {l:'TASKS DONE',v:tasks||0,s:'+0.001 each',c:'#E2C86D',h:'/profile.html'},
   {l:'TIER',v:isOwner?'NEXUS 9':`TIER ${d.membership_tier||1}`,s:'Membership',c:isOwner?'#C9A84C':'#9B6BF0',h:'/vault.html'},
  ].forEach(m=>{
    const el=document.createElement('a');el.className='mb-item';el.href=m.h;el.style.setProperty('--c',m.c);
    el.innerHTML=`<div class="mbi-l">${m.l}</div><div class="mbi-v" style="color:${m.c};font-size:${String(m.v).length>8?'14px':'22px'}">${m.v}</div><div class="mbi-s">${m.s}</div>`;
    g.appendChild(el);
  });

  /* OWNER: Show pending access alert on dashboard */
  if(isOwner){
    let pending=0;
    try{
      const pm=await sb.from('profiles').select('id',{count:'exact',head:true}).eq('access_approved',false).eq('is_owner',false);
      pending=pm.count||0;
    }catch(e){}
    if(pending>0){
      const alert_el=document.getElementById('owner-alert');
      document.getElementById('oa-count').textContent=pending+' MEMBER'+(pending>1?'S':'')+' WAITING';
      alert_el.style.display='block';
    }
  }

  /* If owner but database shows wrong values - fix it */
  if(isOwner&&(parseFloat(d.axis_a||0)<9||!d.access_approved)){
    await sb.from('profiles').update({axis_a:9.000,axis_b:9.000,axis_c:9.000,material_tier:'OMEGA MASTER',access_approved:true,is_trial:false,trial_expires_at:null}).eq('id',uid);
  }

  buildMesh();buildFilms();buildGate();
}
boot();

<script>
/* LIVE CLOCK */
function clock(){
  var n=new Date();
  var days=['SUNDAY','MONDAY','TUESDAY','WEDNESDAY','THURSDAY','FRIDAY','SATURDAY'];
  var months=['JAN','FEB','MAR','APR','MAY','JUN','JUL','AUG','SEP','OCT','NOV','DEC'];
  document.getElementById('sb-date').textContent=days[n.getDay()]+', '+months[n.getMonth()]+' '+n.getDate()+', '+n.getFullYear();
  var h=n.getHours().toString().padStart(2,'0'),m=n.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2,'0'),s=n.getSeconds().toString().padStart(2,'0');
  document.getElementById('sb-time').textContent=h+':'+m+':'+s+' UTC';
}
clock(); setInterval(clock,1000);

/* COUNTDOWN to next Genesis event (Sept 17, 2026 - the &#937; Day) */
function countdown(){
  var target=new Date('2026-09-17T09:17:17Z').getTime();
  function tick(){
    var diff=target-Date.now();
    if(diff<0){ diff=0; }
    var d=Math.floor(diff/86400000);
    var h=Math.floor((diff%86400000)/3600000);
    var m=Math.floor((diff%3600000)/60000);
    var s=Math.floor((diff%60000)/1000);
    document.getElementById('cd-d').textContent=String(d).padStart(2,'0');
    document.getElementById('cd-h').textContent=String(h).padStart(2,'0');
    document.getElementById('cd-m').textContent=String(m).padStart(2,'0');
    document.getElementById('cd-s').textContent=String(s).padStart(2,'0');
  }
  tick(); setInterval(tick,1000);
}
countdown();

/* BACKGROUND: grid lattice + particles */
(function(){
  var cv=document.getElementById('bg-canvas');
  var ctx=cv.getContext('2d');
  var W,H,particles=[];
  function resize(){W=cv.width=window.innerWidth;H=cv.height=window.innerHeight;}
  resize(); window.addEventListener('resize',resize);
  for(var i=0;i<60;i++) particles.push({x:Math.random()*2000,y:Math.random()*1200,vx:(Math.random()-.5)*.3,vy:(Math.random()-.5)*.3,r:Math.random()*1.5+.5,gold:Math.random()>.5});
  var t=0;
  function frame(){
    t+=.004; ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
    /* Grid */
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(201,168,76,0.04)'; ctx.lineWidth=.5;
    var gs=80;
    for(var x=0;x<W;x+=gs){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,0);ctx.lineTo(x,H);ctx.stroke();}
    for(var y=0;y<H;y+=gs){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,y);ctx.lineTo(W,y);ctx.stroke();}
    /* Perspective lines from center */
    var cx=W/2, cy=H/2;
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(201,168,76,0.03)'; ctx.lineWidth=.5;
    for(var a=0;a<12;a++){
      var ang=a*Math.PI/6+t*.1;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(cx,cy); ctx.lineTo(cx+Math.cos(ang)*W,cy+Math.sin(ang)*W); ctx.stroke();
    }
    /* Particles */
    particles.forEach(function(p){
      p.x+=p.vx; p.y+=p.vy;
      if(p.x<0)p.x=W; if(p.x>W)p.x=0; if(p.y<0)p.y=H; if(p.y>H)p.y=0;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x%W,p.y%H,p.r,0,Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fillStyle=p.gold?'rgba(201,168,76,0.5)':'rgba(0,229,255,0.3)'; ctx.fill();
    });
    /* Subtle radial center glow */
    var rg=ctx.createRadialGradient(cx,cy,0,cx,cy,Math.min(W,H)*.4);
    rg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(201,168,76,0.04)'); rg.addColorStop(1,'transparent');
    ctx.fillStyle=rg; ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }
  frame();
})();

/* OMEGA CANVAS: 3D-style gold &#937; with depth rings */
(function(){
  var cv=document.getElementById('omega-canvas');
  var ctx=cv.getContext('2d');
  var W=200,H=140,CX=200,CY=140;
  var t=0;
  function frame(){
    t+=.012; ctx.clearRect(0,0,W*2,H*2);
    /* Glow rings */
    for(var r=0;r<4;r++){
      ctx.save(); ctx.translate(CX,CY+10);
      ctx.rotate(t*.3*(r%2?-1:1)+r*.5);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0,0,W*.7+r*8,H*.25+r*4,0,0,Math.PI*2);
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(201,168,76,'+(0.08-r*.012)+')'; ctx.lineWidth=1; ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    }
    /* Shadow/depth */
    var sg=ctx.createRadialGradient(CX,CY+30,0,CX,CY,120);
    sg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(201,168,76,0.2)'); sg.addColorStop(.6,'rgba(201,168,76,0.04)'); sg.addColorStop(1,'transparent');
    ctx.fillStyle=sg; ctx.fillRect(0,0,W*2,H*2);
    /* Main &#937; */
    var pulse=.85+.15*Math.sin(t*1.8);
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(201,168,76,'+pulse+')';
    ctx.shadowColor='rgba(201,168,76,0.8)'; ctx.shadowBlur=40+20*Math.sin(t*1.2);
    ctx.font='700 '+Math.round(H*1.6)+'px "Cinzel Decorative",serif';
    ctx.textAlign='center'; ctx.textBaseline='middle';
    ctx.fillText('\u03A9',CX,CY-4);
    ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    /* Orbiting dots */
    for(var i=0;i<8;i++){
      var a=t*.8+i*Math.PI/4;
      var rx=W*.82, ry=H*.35;
      var dx=Math.cos(a)*rx, dy=Math.sin(a)*ry;
      var sz=2.5+Math.sin(t*2+i)*1;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(CX+dx,CY+dy,sz,0,Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(201,168,76,'+(0.4+0.5*Math.abs(Math.sin(t+i)))+')';
      ctx.shadowColor='rgba(201,168,76,0.6)'; ctx.shadowBlur=8; ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }
  frame();
})();

/* ECG WAVEFORM: 9.17Hz frequency signature */
(function(){
  var cv=document.getElementById('ecg-canvas');
  var ctx=cv.getContext('2d');
  var t=0;
  function resize(){cv.width=window.innerWidth*2;cv.height=160;cv.style.width=window.innerWidth+'px';cv.style.height='80px';}
  resize(); window.addEventListener('resize',resize);
  function ecgY(x,W,t){
    var cycle=x/W*4+t*.9;
    var pos=cycle%1;
    if(pos<.1) return -Math.sin(pos/0.1*Math.PI)*30;
    if(pos<.12) return Math.sin((pos-.1)/.02*Math.PI*2)*80;
    if(pos<.16) return -Math.sin((pos-.12)/.04*Math.PI)*40;
    if(pos<.2) return Math.sin((pos-.16)/.04*Math.PI)*15;
    return Math.sin(cycle*6.28*2.5)*4;
  }
  function frame(){
    t+=.018;
    var W=cv.width,H=cv.height,CY=H/2;
    ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
    /* Glow */
    var gg=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,H);
    gg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(10,10,15,0)'); gg.addColorStop(.3,'rgba(10,10,15,0.4)'); gg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(10,10,15,0.9)');
    ctx.fillStyle=gg; ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
    /* ECG line */
    ctx.beginPath();
    for(var x=0;x<W;x+=2){
      var y=CY+ecgY(x,W,t);
      x===0?ctx.moveTo(x,y):ctx.lineTo(x,y);
    }
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(201,168,76,0.7)'; ctx.lineWidth=2;
    ctx.shadowColor='rgba(201,168,76,0.5)'; ctx.shadowBlur=8; ctx.stroke(); ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    /* Moving dot on ecg */
    var headX=(t*.8%1)*W;
    var headY=CY+ecgY(headX,W,t);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(headX,headY,4,0,Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fillStyle='#C9A84C'; ctx.shadowColor='#C9A84C'; ctx.shadowBlur=16; ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }
  frame();
})();

/* ENTER GATEWAY: biometric sequence then auth */
document.getElementById('btn-enter').addEventListener('click',function(){
  var overlay=document.getElementById('bio-overlay');
  overlay.classList.add('show');
  var labels=['SCANNING SOVEREIGN CREDENTIALS...','PALM_PRINT: VERIFIED','RETL_SCAN_MATCH','BIOMETRIC_DATA_HASH: CONFIRMED','INITIATING VAULT ACCESS...'];
  var step=0;
  var interval=setInterval(function(){
    step++;
    if(step<labels.length){
      document.getElementById('bio-sub').textContent=labels[step];
    } else {
      clearInterval(interval);
      window.location.href='/account.html';
    }
  },700);
});
</script>

<script type="module">
import{createClient}from'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2';
const sb=createClient("https://ydqhzvvoyufiiqvzcjns.supabase.co","sb_publishable_9KlhhnvRs4OKgw6nxXHmYw_GxszJ46q");
let uid=null;let members=[];

/* Children's Trust Timer - target: 18 years from a child's birth */
function updateTrustTimer(){
  /* Reference: Youngest child reaches 18 - approximate target 2034 */
  const target=new Date('2034-01-01');
  const now=new Date();
  const diff=target-now;
  if(diff<0){document.getElementById('trust-timer').textContent='MATURITY REACHED';return;}
  const years=Math.floor(diff/(1000*60*60*24*365.25));
  const months=Math.floor((diff%(1000*60*60*24*365.25))/(1000*60*60*24*30.44));
  document.getElementById('trust-timer').textContent=years+' YEARS '+months+' MONTHS';
  document.getElementById('trust-sub').textContent='Founder: Major Sleiman Youssef Dagher . Locked until maturity';
  const pct=Math.round((1-(diff/(new Date('2034-01-01')-new Date('2020-01-01'))))*100);
  document.getElementById('trust-bar').style.width=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,pct))+'%';
}
updateTrustTimer();setInterval(updateTrustTimer,60000);

/* Allowance mini chart */
(function(){
  const cv=document.getElementById('allowance-cv');
  const W=cv.width,H=cv.height,ctx=cv.getContext('2d');let t=0;
  const data=[42,55,38,71,60,85,91];
  function f(){
    t+=.02;ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
    const step=W/(data.length-1);
    ctx.beginPath();
    data.forEach((d,i)=>{const x=i*step,y=H-(d/100)*H*.85;i===0?ctx.moveTo(x,y):ctx.lineTo(x,y);});
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(63,178,127,0.7)';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.shadowColor='rgba(63,178,127,.5)';ctx.shadowBlur=6;ctx.stroke();ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    /* Points */
    data.forEach((d,i)=>{
      const x=i*step,y=H-(d/100)*H*.85;
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y+(1.5*Math.sin(t+i)),3,0,Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fillStyle='#3fb27f';ctx.shadowColor='#3fb27f';ctx.shadowBlur=6;ctx.fill();ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    });
    requestAnimationFrame(f);}f();
})();

/* Constellation canvas */
function drawConstellation(mems){
  const cv=document.getElementById('con-cv');
  const W=cv.offsetWidth||800;const H=260;
  cv.width=W*2;cv.height=H*2;cv.style.width=W+'px';cv.style.height=H+'px';
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d');ctx.scale(2,2);let t=0;
  const CX=W/2,CY=H/2;
  const center={x:CX,y:CY,name:'SLEIMAN',role:'ARCHITECT',col:'#C9A84C',isRoot:true};
  const COLS={Wife:'#9B6BF0',Son:'#00E5FF',Daughter:'#3fb27f',Mother:'#D9B86A',Father:'#D9B86A',Brother:'#C7CDD6',Sister:'#A9C2D8'};
  const nodes=[center,...mems.map((m,i)=>{const a=-Math.PI/2+i*(Math.PI*2/Math.max(mems.length,1));const r=Math.min(W,H)*.3;return{x:CX+Math.cos(a)*r,y:CY+Math.sin(a)*r,name:m.name?.split(' ')[0]||'NODE',role:m.relation||'FAMILY',col:COLS[m.relation]||'#C9A84C',isHeir:m.is_heir};})]
  function frame(){
    t+=.01;ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
    /* Stars */
    for(let i=0;i<30;i++){const sx=(i*61+t*3)%W,sy=(i*43+t*2)%H;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(sx,sy,.8,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fillStyle='rgba(201,168,76,'+(0.04+0.08*Math.sin(t+i))+')';ctx.fill();}
    /* Lines */
    nodes.forEach((n,i)=>{if(i===0)return;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(center.x,center.y);ctx.lineTo(n.x,n.y);ctx.strokeStyle=n.col+'33';ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.setLineDash([4,7]);ctx.stroke();ctx.setLineDash([]);});
    /* Nodes */
    nodes.forEach((n,i)=>{
      const px=n.x+Math.cos(t*.5+i)*2,py=n.y+Math.sin(t*.7+i)*1.5;
      const r=n.isRoot?20:n.isHeir?14:11;
      const gg=ctx.createRadialGradient(px,py,0,px,py,r+8);gg.addColorStop(0,n.col+'44');gg.addColorStop(1,'transparent');
      ctx.fillStyle=gg;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(px,py,r+8,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(px,py,r,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.strokeStyle=n.col+(n.isRoot?'cc':'88');ctx.lineWidth=n.isRoot?2:1.5;ctx.shadowColor=n.col;ctx.shadowBlur=n.isRoot?16:8;ctx.stroke();ctx.shadowBlur=0;
      ctx.fillStyle=n.col;ctx.shadowColor=n.col;ctx.shadowBlur=8;
      ctx.font='700 '+(n.isRoot?14:10)+'px "Cinzel Decorative",serif';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';ctx.fillText('\u03A9',px,py+1);ctx.shadowBlur=0;
      ctx.fillStyle=n.col;ctx.font='600 7px "Courier Prime",monospace';ctx.fillText(n.name,px,py+r+7);
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(133,131,123,0.6)';ctx.font='6px "Courier Prime",monospace';ctx.fillText(n.role.toUpperCase(),px,py+r+15);
    });
    requestAnimationFrame(frame);}frame();
}

function buildMemberCards(rows){
  const g=document.getElementById('member-grid');g.innerHTML='';
  if(!rows.length){g.innerHTML='<div style="grid-column:1/-1;text-align:center;padding:24px;font-family:var(--M);font-size:11px;color:var(--muted)">No family nodes registered yet. Add your first bloodline node below.</div>';return;}
  rows.forEach(m=>{
    const c=document.createElement('div');c.className='mc'+(m.is_heir?' heir':'');
    c.innerHTML=`<div class="mc-rel">${m.relation?.toUpperCase()||'FAMILY'}</div><div class="mc-name">${m.name}</div><div class="mc-sign">${m.sign||'SIGN NOT SET'}</div><div class="mc-actions"><button class="mc-btn mc-heir-btn" data-id="${m.id}" data-heir="${m.is_heir?1:0}">${m.is_heir?'REVOKE HEIR':'SET HEIR'}</button><button class="mc-btn mc-del" data-id="${m.id}">REMOVE</button></div>`;
    c.querySelector('.mc-heir-btn').addEventListener('click',async e=>{const id=e.target.dataset.id;const h=e.target.dataset.heir==='1';if(!h){await sb.from('family_nodes').update({is_heir:false}).eq('user_id',uid);await sb.from('family_nodes').update({is_heir:true}).eq('id',id).eq('user_id',uid);}else{await sb.from('family_nodes').update({is_heir:false}).eq('id',id).eq('user_id',uid);}boot();});
    c.querySelector('.mc-del').addEventListener('click',async e=>{if(confirm('Remove node?')){await sb.from('family_nodes').delete().eq('id',e.target.dataset.id).eq('user_id',uid);boot();}});
    g.appendChild(c);
  });
}

document.getElementById('btn-add').addEventListener('click',async()=>{
  const n=document.getElementById('af-name').value.trim();
  const r=document.getElementById('af-rel').value;
  if(!n||!r){alert('Name and relation required.');return;}
  const {error}=await sb.from('family_nodes').insert({user_id:uid,name:n,relation:r,sign:document.getElementById('af-sign').value||null,is_heir:false});
  if(!error){document.getElementById('af-name').value='';document.getElementById('af-rel').value='';const msg=document.getElementById('add-msg');msg.style.display='block';setTimeout(()=>msg.style.display='none',3000);boot();}
});

async function boot(){
  const sess=(await sb.auth.getSession()).data.session;
  if(!sess){window.location.href='/account.html';return;}
  uid=sess.user.id;
  const {data:rows}=await sb.from('family_nodes').select('*').eq('user_id',uid).order('created_at',{ascending:true});
  members=rows||[];
  buildMemberCards(members);
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>drawConstellation(members));
}
boot();
</script>

</body>
</html>

    <div class="topbar"><div class="t"><span data-i18n="gaming.eyebrow">THE GAMES</span><small>12 GAMES  .  12 STAGES  .  9x9x9 GATED</small></div></div>
    <p class="intro">Twelve games, each with twelve stages  --  <b>144 nodes of mastery</b>. No stage is given. Clear the <b>Sequence Lock</b> exactly to take a node; each cleared node raises your <b data-i18n="matrix.mastery_t">Mastery</b> and so your level, grade, and trophies. A game opens only when your matrix is ready and the one before it is broken.</p>
