<script type="module">
  import { createClient } from 'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2';
  const supabase = createClient("https://ydqhzvvoyufiiqvzcjns.supabase.co","sb_publishable_9KlhhnvRs4OKgw6nxXHmYw_GxszJ46q");
  let user=null, axisB=1, cleared={}, selGame=-1;

  const GAMES=[['Aries','Ares'],['Taurus','Aphrodite'],['Gemini','Hermes'],['Cancer','Artemis'],['Leo','Apollo'],['Virgo','Athena'],['Libra','Hera'],['Scorpio','Demeter'],['Sagittarius','Zeus'],['Capricorn','Hestia'],['Aquarius','Hephaestus'],['Pisces','Poseidon']];
  const ROMAN=['I','II','III','IV','V','VI','VII','VIII','IX','X','XI','XII'];
  function key(g,s){ return 'g'+g+'s'+s; }
  function isCleared(g,s){ return !!cleared[key(g,s)]; }
  function clearsIn(g){ var n=0; for(var s=0;s<12;s++) if(isCleared(g,s)) n++; return n; }
  function gameUnlocked(g){ if(g===0) return true; return clearsIn(g-1)>=6 && axisB>=g; }
  function subUnlocked(g,s){ if(!gameUnlocked(g)) return false; return s===0 || isCleared(g,s-1); }

  function sleep(ms){ return new Promise(function(r){ setTimeout(r,ms); }); }

  function showGate(h,p){ document.getElementById('gate-h').textContent=h; document.getElementById('gate-p').textContent=p; document.getElementById('gate').style.display='block'; document.getElementById('app').style.display='none'; }

  function renderGames(){
    var box=document.getElementById('games'); box.innerHTML='';
    GAMES.forEach(function(gm,g){
      var unlocked=gameUnlocked(g), n=clearsIn(g);
      var d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='game'+(unlocked?'':' locked')+(g===selGame?' sel':'');
      var lock=unlocked?'':'<div class="lock">LOCKED  .  need Mastery \u2265 '+g+' and 6 stages cleared in Game '+ROMAN[g-1]+'</div>';
      d.innerHTML='<div class="gi">GAME '+ROMAN[g]+' \u00B7 '+gm[0].toUpperCase()+'</div><div class="gn">'+gm[1]+'\u2019s Gauntlet</div><div class="gs">'+n+' / 12 CLEARED</div><div class="bar"><i style="width:'+(n/12*100)+'%"></i></div>'+lock;
      if(unlocked) d.addEventListener('click',function(){ openGame(g); });
      box.appendChild(d);
    });
  }

  function openGame(g){
    selGame=g; renderGames();
    var st=document.getElementById('stages'); st.classList.add('open');
    document.getElementById('st-name').textContent=GAMES[g][1]+'\u2019s Gauntlet \u2014 Game '+ROMAN[g];
    document.getElementById('st-sub').textContent='Clear each node in order. One mistake fails the stage.';
    var nb=document.getElementById('st-nodes'); nb.innerHTML='';
    for(var s=0;s<12;s++){ (function(s){
      var cl=isCleared(g,s), av=subUnlocked(g,s) && !cl, avail=subUnlocked(g,s);
      var n=document.createElement('div'); n.className='node'+(cl?' cleared':(av?' avail':''));
      n.textContent=ROMAN[s];
      if(avail) n.addEventListener('click',function(){ launch(g,s); });
      nb.appendChild(n);
    })(s); }
    st.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'nearest'});
  }

  // ===== Sequence Lock =====
  let seq=[], target=[], input=[], busy=false, curG=-1, curS=-1, reverse=false, interval=600;
  function buildGrid(){
    var g=document.getElementById('m-grid'); g.innerHTML='';
    for(var i=0;i<9;i++){ (function(i){
      var c=document.createElement('div'); c.className='cell'; c.setAttribute('data-i',i);
      c.addEventListener('click',function(){ press(i); });
      g.appendChild(c);
    })(i); }
  }
  function cellEl(i){ return document.querySelector('.cell[data-i="'+i+'"]'); }
  async function flash(i,cls){ var el=cellEl(i); el.classList.add(cls||'lit'); await sleep(Math.max(160,interval*0.55)); el.classList.remove(cls||'lit'); }
  function setStat(t){ document.getElementById('m-stat').textContent=t; }

  function launch(g,s){
    curG=g; curS=s;
    var d=g*12+s;
    var L=3+Math.floor(d/7);                 // 3 .. ~23
    interval=Math.max(240, 640 - d*3);       // faster as you climb
    reverse=g>=6;                            // upper half: reverse order
    seq=[]; for(var i=0;i<L;i++) seq.push(Math.floor(Math.random()*9));
    document.getElementById('m-title').textContent='GAME '+ROMAN[g]+' \u00B7 STAGE '+ROMAN[s];
    document.getElementById('m-diff').textContent='LENGTH '+L+' \u00B7 '+(reverse?'REVERSE ORDER':'FORWARD')+' \u00B7 '+(isCleared(g,s)?'REPLAY':'NEW NODE');
    setStat('Press WATCH to see the sequence.');
    document.getElementById('m-action').textContent='WATCH';
    document.getElementById('m-action').disabled=false;
    document.getElementById('modal').classList.add('open');
  }

  async function watch(){
    busy=true; input=[]; document.getElementById('m-action').disabled=true;
    target=reverse?seq.slice().reverse():seq.slice();
    setStat('WATCH...'); await sleep(500);
    for(var i=0;i<seq.length;i++){ await flash(seq[i]); await sleep(Math.max(90,interval*0.35)); }
    busy=false; setStat(reverse?'YOUR TURN \u2014 REPEAT IN REVERSE':'YOUR TURN \u2014 REPEAT THE SEQUENCE');
  }

  async function press(i){
    if(busy) return;
    if(!target.length) return;
    var k=input.length;
    if(i!==target[k]){
      busy=true; await flash(i,'miss');
      setStat('THE NODE REJECTS YOU. Press RETRY.');
      document.getElementById('m-action').textContent='RETRY';
      document.getElementById('m-action').disabled=false; target=[]; busy=false; return;
    }
    await flash(i);
    input.push(i);
    if(input.length===target.length){ await cleared_(); }
  }

  async function cleared_(){
    busy=true; setStat('NODE CLEARED \u2014 awarding Mastery...');
    var already=isCleared(curG,curS);
    cleared[key(curG,curS)]=true;
    try{ await supabase.rpc('complete_task',{p_kind:'gaming',p_task:key(curG,curS),p_axis:'b',p_title:'Game '+(curG+1)+' Stage '+(curS+1)}); }catch(e){}
    if(!already){ axisB=Math.min(9, axisB+0.25); }
    setStat(already?'NODE ALREADY YOURS \u2014 well struck.':'NODE CLEARED \u2014 +0.25 MASTERY.');
    document.getElementById('m-action').textContent='CONTINUE';
    document.getElementById('m-action').disabled=false;
    busy=false;
    renderGames(); openGame(curG); updateMStat();
  }

  function actionClick(){
    var lbl=document.getElementById('m-action').textContent;
    if(lbl==='WATCH'||lbl==='RETRY') watch();
    else if(lbl==='CONTINUE'){ document.getElementById('modal').classList.remove('open'); }
  }

  function updateMStat(){
    var total=0; for(var g=0;g<12;g++) total+=clearsIn(g);
    document.getElementById('mstat').innerHTML='MASTERY (B) <b>'+axisB.toFixed(2)+'</b> / 9 \u00B7 NODES CLEARED <b>'+total+'</b> / 144';
  }

  async function boot(){
    var sess=(await supabase.auth.getSession()).data.session;
    if(!sess){ showGate('The Games','Sign in to enter the games.'); return; }
    user=sess.user;
    var pr=await supabase.from('profiles').select('sign,axis_b').eq('id',user.id).maybeSingle();
    if(!pr.data || !pr.data.sign){ showGate('Set Your Cosmology','Set your cosmology first to enter the games.'); return; }
    axisB=Number(pr.data.axis_b||1);
    try{
      var tc=await supabase.from('task_completions').select('task').eq('user_id',user.id).eq('kind','gaming');
      if(tc.data) tc.data.forEach(function(r){ cleared[r.task]=true; });
    }catch(e){}
    document.getElementById('gate').style.display='none';
    document.getElementById('app').style.display='flex';
    buildGrid(); renderGames(); updateMStat();
  }
  document.getElementById('m-action').addEventListener('click',actionClick);
  document.getElementById('m-close')?.addEventListener('click',function(){ document.getElementById('modal').classList.remove('open'); });
  document.getElementById('logout')?.addEventListener('click',async function(){ await supabase.auth.signOut(); window.location.href='/account.html'; });
  boot();
</script>
<script src="/nav.js"></script>
<script src="/bg.js"></script>
</body>

<script type="module">
  import { createClient } from 'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2';
  const supabase = createClient(
    "https://ydqhzvvoyufiiqvzcjns.supabase.co",
    "sb_publishable_9KlhhnvRs4OKgw6nxXHmYw_GxszJ46q"
  );
  const ELEM_ORDER=['Fire','Water','Wind','Metal','Sand'];
  function esc(x){return String(x==null?'':x).replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]));}

  async function load(){
    const status=document.getElementById('h-status');
    try{
      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('order_stats');
      if(error){ status.textContent='Could not read the record ('+(error.code||error.message||'')+').'; console.log('order_stats error',error); return; }
      const d = data || {};
      document.getElementById('h-members').textContent = (d.members ?? 0);
      document.getElementById('h-certs').textContent = (d.certificates ?? 0);
      document.getElementById('h-events').textContent = (d.events ?? 0);
      const els = d.elements || {};
      let max=1; ELEM_ORDER.forEach(e=>{ if((els[e]||0)>max) max=(els[e]||0); });
      const box=document.getElementById('h-elements'); box.innerHTML='';
      ELEM_ORDER.forEach(e=>{
        const n=els[e]||0;
        const row=document.createElement('div'); row.className='brow';
        const bl=document.createElement('span'); bl.className='bl'; bl.textContent=e.toUpperCase();
        const track=document.createElement('span'); track.className='track';
        const fill=document.createElement('span'); fill.className='fill'; fill.style.width=Math.round((n/max)*100)+'%';
        track.appendChild(fill);
        const bn=document.createElement('span'); bn.className='bn'; bn.textContent=n;
        row.appendChild(bl); row.appendChild(track); row.appendChild(bn); box.appendChild(row);
      });

      // --- Hall of Fame: ranked ascendants ---
      try{
        const APEX=15.588;
        const { data: board, error: berr } = await supabase.rpc('public_leaderboard', { p_limit: 25 });
        const box=document.getElementById('h-board');
        if(berr){ box.innerHTML='<div class="lmeta">The roll could not be read.</div>'; }
        else if(!board || !board.length){ box.innerHTML='<div class="lmeta">No ascendants yet. Be the first to cross the threshold.</div>'; }
        else {
          box.innerHTML='';
          board.forEach(m=>{
            const row=document.createElement('div'); row.className='lrow'+(m.is_owner?' owner':'');
            const pct=Math.max(3,Math.min(100,Math.round((Number(m.authority||0)/APEX)*100)));
            const sign=esc(m.sign||'UNSEALED'); const elem=m.element?(' . '+esc(m.element)):'';
            const creds=[]; if(m.certificates)creds.push(m.certificates+' CERT'); if(m.trophies)creds.push(m.trophies+' TRO'); if(m.medals)creds.push(m.medals+' MED');
            row.innerHTML=
              '<div class="lrank">'+m.rank+'</div>'+
              '<div><div class="lname">'+esc(m.name)+(m.is_owner?'<span class="tag">SOVEREIGN</span>':'')+'</div>'+
              '<div class="lmeta"><span class="s">'+sign+'</span>'+elem+'  .  ('+m.axis_a+', '+m.axis_b+', '+m.axis_c+')</div>'+
              (creds.length?'<div class="lcreds">'+creds.join('   .   ')+'</div>':'')+
              '</div>'+
              '<div class="lauth"><span class="v">'+Number(m.authority).toFixed(3)+'</span><div class="abar"><div class="afill" style="width:'+pct+'%"></div></div></div>';
            box.appendChild(row);
          });
        }
      }catch(e){ console.log('leaderboard',e); }

      status.textContent='';
    }catch(e){ status.textContent='Could not read the record.'; console.log(e); }
  }
  load();
</script>
<script src="/bg.js"></script>

<script>
document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(btn=>{
  btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-panel').forEach(p=>p.classList.remove('active'));
    btn.classList.add('active');
    document.getElementById('tab-'+btn.dataset.tab)?.classList.add('active');
  });
});
if(location.hash){document.querySelector('[data-tab="'+location.hash.replace('#','')+'"]')?.click();}
/* Toggle widget */
function makeToggle(on){
  const el=document.createElement('div');el.className='toggle'+(on?' on':'');
  el.addEventListener('click',()=>{el.classList.toggle('on');});
  return el;
}
</script>
<script type="module">
import{createClient}from'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2';
const sb=createClient("https://ydqhzvvoyufiiqvzcjns.supabase.co","sb_publishable_9KlhhnvRs4OKgw6nxXHmYw_GxszJ46q");

const MATS=['SAND','GLASS','IRON','STEEL','TITANIUM','CARBON','GOLD','PLATINUM','OMEGA'];
const MAT_COLS=['#D9B86A','#A9C2D8','#C7CDD6','#8B8F9C','#607D8B','#455A64','#C9A84C','#E8D5B7','#C9A84C'];
const KYC_STEPS=[

function drawCharCanvas(sign){
  const cv=document.getElementById('char-cv');if(!cv)return;
  const ch=CH_MAP[sign]||CH_MAP.Virgo;
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d'),CX=160,CY=160;let t=0;
  const GLYPHS={Aries:'\u2648',Taurus:'\u2649',Gemini:'\u264A',Cancer:'\u264B',Leo:'\u264C',Virgo:'\u264D',Libra:'\u264E',Scorpio:'\u264F',Sagittarius:'\u2650',Capricorn:'\u2651',Aquarius:'\u2652',Pisces:'\u2653'};
  function frame(){
    t+=.02;ctx.clearRect(0,0,320,320);
    const bg=ctx.createRadialGradient(CX,CY,0,CX,CY,CX*.85);
    bg.addColorStop(0,ch.col+'22');bg.addColorStop(1,'transparent');
    ctx.fillStyle=bg;ctx.fillRect(0,0,320,320);
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(CX,CY);
    ctx.save();ctx.rotate(t*.3);
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,CX*.75,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.strokeStyle=ch.col+'44';ctx.lineWidth=1.5;ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.save();ctx.rotate(-t*.2);
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,CX*.7,CX*.26,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.strokeStyle=ch.col+'33';ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.fillStyle=ch.col;ctx.shadowColor=ch.col;ctx.shadowBlur=18+5*Math.sin(t*1.5);
    ctx.font='700 60px "Cinzel Decorative",serif';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';
    ctx.fillText(GLYPHS[sign]||'\u03A9',0,2);ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    ctx.restore();requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }
  frame();
}

function buildKYC(kyc){
  const g=document.getElementById('kyc-steps');g.innerHTML='';
  const done=kyc==='verified'?7:kyc==='submitted'?5:2;
  KYC_STEPS.forEach((s,i)=>{
    const isDone=i<done,isActive=i===done;
    const col=isDone?'var(--green)':isActive?'var(--gold)':'rgba(133,131,123,0.3)';
    const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='kyc-step';
    d.innerHTML=`<div class="ks-circle" style="border-color:${col};color:${col}${isActive?';box-shadow:0 0 14px rgba(201,168,76,0.3)':''}">${isDone?'&#x2713;':s.n}</div><div class="ks-body"><div class="ksb-title" style="color:${isDone?'var(--green)':isActive?'var(--solar)':'rgba(233,230,220,0.4)'}">${s.title}</div><div class="ksb-desc">${s.desc}</div>${isActive&&s.action?'<button class="ks-action" style="color:var(--gold);border-color:rgba(201,168,76,.4)">'+(s.action)+'</button>':''}</div>`;
    g.appendChild(d);
  });
}

function buildEvoStrip(tier){
  const g=document.getElementById('evo-strip');g.innerHTML='';
  MATS.forEach((m,i)=>{
    const done=i<tier,active=i===tier-1;
    const c=document.createElement('div');
    c.style.cssText=`display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:5px;min-width:70px;padding:0 4px;flex-shrink:0`;
    c.innerHTML=`<div style="width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:50%;border:2px solid ${done||active?MAT_COLS[i]:MAT_COLS[i]+'33'};display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-family:var(--D);font-size:10px;color:${done||active?MAT_COLS[i]:'rgba(133,131,123,0.3)'}${active?';box-shadow:0 0 12px '+MAT_COLS[i]+'55':''}">${i+1}</div><div style="font-family:var(--M);font-size:7px;letter-spacing:1px;color:${active?MAT_COLS[i]:'var(--muted)'};text-align:center">${m}</div>`;
    if(i<MATS.length-1) c.innerHTML+='<div style="position:absolute;right:-12px;top:15px;font-family:var(--M);font-size:10px;color:rgba(201,168,76,0.2)">--</div>';
    g.appendChild(c);
  });
}

function buildSettings(){
  const g=document.getElementById('settings-grid');g.innerHTML='';
  SETTINGS_DATA.forEach(sec=>{
    const c=document.createElement('div');c.className='sg-card';
    c.innerHTML=`<div class="sgc-title">${sec.title}</div>`;
    sec.items.forEach(item=>{
      const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='toggle-row';
      const toggle=makeToggle(item[1]==='on');
      const label=document.createElement('span');label.className='tr-label';label.textContent=item[0];
      row.appendChild(label);row.appendChild(toggle);
      c.appendChild(row);
    });
    g.appendChild(c);
  });
}

function buildBarcode(){
  const bc=document.getElementById('pp-barcode');if(!bc)return;
  bc.innerHTML='';
  for(let i=0;i<80;i++){
    const b=document.createElement('div');b.className='bar';
    const h=Math.random()>.5?(8+Math.random()*16):(3+Math.random()*8);
    b.style.cssText=`flex:${Math.random()>.7?2:1};height:${h}px;align-self:flex-end`;
    bc.appendChild(b);
  }
}

async function boot(){
  const sess=(await sb.auth.getSession()).data.session;
  if(!sess){window.location.href='/account.html';return;}
  const uid=sess.user.id;
  const pr=await sb.from('profiles').select('*').eq('id',uid).maybeSingle();
  const d=pr.data||{};
  const a=parseFloat(d.axis_a)||1,b=parseFloat(d.axis_b)||1,c=parseFloat(d.axis_c)||1;
  const auth=Math.sqrt(a*a+b*b+c*c);
  const sign=d.sign||'Virgo';
  const tier=Math.max(1,Math.min(9,Math.floor(Math.min(a,b,c))));
  const el=EL_MAP[sign]||'SAND';
  const ch=CH_MAP[sign]||CH_MAP.Virgo;

  /* Profile tab */
  const name=d.display_name||'SOVEREIGN';
  document.getElementById('av-init').textContent=name[0]?.toUpperCase()||'S';
  document.getElementById('pcm-name').textContent=name.toUpperCase();
  document.getElementById('pcm-role').textContent=(d.is_owner?'ARCHITECT . OWNER . ':'')+sign.toUpperCase()+' . '+el+' . LEVEL '+a.toFixed(1);
  document.getElementById('ps-auth').textContent=auth.toFixed(2);
  document.getElementById('ps-pos').textContent='('+a.toFixed(1)+','+b.toFixed(1)+','+c.toFixed(1)+')';
  document.getElementById('ps-sign').textContent=sign.toUpperCase();
  document.getElementById('ps-el').textContent=el;
  setTimeout(()=>{
    document.getElementById('bar-a').style.width=(a/9*100)+'%';
    document.getElementById('bar-b').style.width=(b/9*100)+'%';
    document.getElementById('bar-c').style.width=(c/9*100)+'%';
  },300);
  document.getElementById('val-a').textContent=a.toFixed(1);
  document.getElementById('val-b').textContent=b.toFixed(1);
  document.getElementById('val-c').textContent=c.toFixed(1);
  document.getElementById('ef-name').value=d.display_name||'';
  document.getElementById('ef-sign').value=d.sign||'';
  var _n=document.getElementById('ef-nation'); if(_n) _n.value=d.nationality||'';
  var _p=document.getElementById('ef-prof');   if(_p) _p.value=d.profession||'';

  /* Passport tab */
  document.getElementById('pp-id').textContent='SYD-OMEGA-91717 . ID: '+uid.substring(0,8).toUpperCase();
  document.getElementById('ppf-name').textContent=name.toUpperCase();
  document.getElementById('ppf-sign').textContent=sign.toUpperCase()+' . '+el;
  document.getElementById('ppf-pos').textContent='('+a.toFixed(1)+','+b.toFixed(1)+','+c.toFixed(1)+')';
  document.getElementById('ppf-auth').textContent=auth.toFixed(2);
  document.getElementById('ppf-date').textContent=new Date(d.created_at||Date.now()).toLocaleDateString('en-US',{year:'numeric',month:'long',day:'numeric'}).toUpperCase();
  buildBarcode();

  /* KYC tab */
  buildKYC(d.kyc_status||'not_started');

  /* Character tab */
  document.getElementById('char-type').textContent=sign.toUpperCase()+' . '+el+' ARCHETYPE';
  document.getElementById('char-name').textContent=ch.name;
  document.getElementById('char-role').textContent=ch.role;
  document.getElementById('char-desc').textContent=ch.desc;
  document.querySelectorAll('.sv, .pcm-name').forEach(el2=>{});
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>drawCharCanvas(sign));
  buildEvoStrip(tier);
  buildSettings();

  /* Save profile */
  document.getElementById('btn-profile-save')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{
    const n=document.getElementById('ef-name').value.trim();
    const sg=document.getElementById('ef-sign').value;
    const nat=document.getElementById('ef-nation').value.trim();
    const prof=document.getElementById('ef-prof').value.trim();
    if(!n) return;
    await sb.from('profiles').update({display_name:n,sign:sg||sign,nationality:nat,profession:prof,updated_at:new Date().toISOString()}).eq('id',uid);
    const m=document.getElementById('profile-save-msg');m.style.display='block';setTimeout(()=>m.style.display='none',3000);
    document.getElementById('pcm-name').textContent=n.toUpperCase();
  });

  document.getElementById('btn-settings-save')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{
    const m=document.getElementById('settings-save-msg');m.style.display='block';setTimeout(()=>m.style.display='none',3000);
  });
}
boot();
</script>

<script>
/* ===== STAR FIELD ===== */
(function(){
  const cv=document.getElementById('star-cv');
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d');let t=0;
  const stars=Array.from({length:200},()=>({x:Math.random(),y:Math.random(),r:Math.random()*1.4+.2,s:Math.random()*.4+.06,col:Math.random()>.8?'rgba(0,229,255,':'rgba(201,168,76,'}));
  function resize(){cv.width=cv.offsetWidth;cv.height=cv.offsetHeight;}
  resize();window.addEventListener('resize',resize);
  function frame(){
    t+=.005;ctx.clearRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
    stars.forEach(s=>{
      const opa=0.04+0.14*Math.sin(t*s.s*4+s.x*8);
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(s.x*cv.width,s.y*cv.height,s.r*(0.6+0.4*Math.sin(t*s.s*2)),0,Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fillStyle=s.col+opa+')';ctx.fill();
    });
    /* Nebula gradient */
    const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(cv.width*.5,cv.height*.4,0,cv.width*.5,cv.height*.4,cv.width*.6);
    g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(201,168,76,0.03)');g.addColorStop(.5,'rgba(0,229,255,0.015)');g.addColorStop(1,'transparent');
    ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }
  frame();
})();

/* ===== 9-DIMENSIONAL OMEGA LOGO ===== */
(function(){
  const cv=document.getElementById('omega-9d');
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d');
  let t=0;
  /* Canvas physical size */
  function resize(){
    const s=cv.parentElement.offsetWidth||400;
    cv.width=s*2;cv.height=s*2;cv.style.width=s+'px';cv.style.height=s+'px';
  }
  resize();window.addEventListener('resize',resize);

  /* 9 Dimensions - each a ring with unique properties */
  const DIMS=[
    {name:'SAND',     col:'#D9B86A',r:0.44,speed:0.12, tilt:0.0,  w:1.2, dash:[]},
    {name:'GLASS',    col:'#A9C2D8',r:0.41,speed:-0.18,tilt:0.35, w:1.0, dash:[8,10]},
    {name:'IRON',     col:'#C7CDD6',r:0.37,speed:0.22, tilt:0.70, w:1.0, dash:[]},
    {name:'STEEL',    col:'#8B8F9C',r:0.33,speed:-0.28,tilt:1.05, w:0.9, dash:[4,8]},
    {name:'TITANIUM', col:'#607D8B',r:0.29,speed:0.35, tilt:1.40, w:0.9, dash:[]},
    {name:'CARBON',   col:'#00E5FF',r:0.25,speed:-0.44,tilt:1.75, w:1.2, dash:[6,6]},
    {name:'GOLD',     col:'#C9A84C',r:0.20,speed:0.55, tilt:2.10, w:1.5, dash:[]},
    {name:'PLATINUM', col:'#E8D5B7',r:0.15,speed:-0.70,tilt:2.45, w:1.5, dash:[3,5]},
    {name:'OMEGA',    col:'#FFD700',r:0.09,speed:0.90, tilt:2.80, w:2.0, dash:[]},
  ];

  /* Particles on each ring */
  const particles=DIMS.map((d,di)=>
    Array.from({length:3+di%3},(_,i)=>({angle:i*Math.PI*2/(3+di%3),speed:d.speed*(0.8+Math.random()*.4)}))
  );

  function drawRing(d,idx,t,CX,CY,S){
    const r=d.r*S;
    /* Perspective tilt: squish into ellipse */
    const tiltAngle=d.tilt+t*0.03;
    const b=Math.abs(Math.cos(tiltAngle))*r;
    const rot=t*d.speed*0.04+d.tilt*0.5;

    ctx.save();ctx.translate(CX,CY);ctx.rotate(rot);
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,r,Math.max(2,b),0,0,Math.PI*2);
    const alpha=0.25+0.25*Math.sin(t*.4+idx*.7);
    ctx.strokeStyle=d.col+Math.floor(alpha*255).toString(16).padStart(2,'0');
    ctx.lineWidth=d.w;
    if(d.dash.length) ctx.setLineDash(d.dash); else ctx.setLineDash([]);
    ctx.shadowColor=d.col;ctx.shadowBlur=idx<6?4:8;
    ctx.stroke();ctx.shadowBlur=0;ctx.setLineDash([]);

    /* Particles on ring */
    particles[idx].forEach(p=>{
      p.angle+=p.speed*0.012;
      const px=Math.cos(p.angle)*r;
      const py=Math.sin(p.angle)*Math.max(2,b);
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(px,py,idx<6?2:3,0,Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fillStyle=d.col;ctx.shadowColor=d.col;ctx.shadowBlur=8;ctx.fill();ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    });
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function frame(){
    t+=0.4;
    const W=cv.width,H=cv.height,CX=W/2,CY=H/2,S=Math.min(W,H);
    ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);

    /* Deep space core glow */
    const cg=ctx.createRadialGradient(CX,CY,0,CX,CY,S*.18);
    cg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(201,168,76,0.15)');
    cg.addColorStop(.5,'rgba(0,229,255,0.06)');
    cg.addColorStop(1,'transparent');
    ctx.fillStyle=cg;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(CX,CY,S*.18,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();

    /* Draw 9 rings outer to inner */
    for(let i=0;i<9;i++) drawRing(DIMS[i],i,t,CX,CY,S);

    /* Frequency wave ripples */
    for(let w=1;w<=3;w++){
      const wr=(S*.05)*(1+((t*0.5+w*30)%60)/60)*2;
      const wa=Math.max(0,0.3-wr/(S*.12));
      if(wa>0){
        ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(CX,CY,wr,0,Math.PI*2);
        ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(0,229,255,'+wa+')';ctx.lineWidth=.7;ctx.stroke();
      }
    }

    /* Central Omega glyph */
    const pulse=0.85+0.15*Math.sin(t*.08);
    const innerGlow=ctx.createRadialGradient(CX,CY,0,CX,CY,S*.08);
    innerGlow.addColorStop(0,'rgba(201,168,76,0.5)');
    innerGlow.addColorStop(.6,'rgba(201,168,76,0.15)');
    innerGlow.addColorStop(1,'transparent');
    ctx.fillStyle=innerGlow;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(CX,CY,S*.08,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();

    const fs=Math.round(S*.16);
    ctx.font='900 '+fs+'px "Cinzel Decorative",serif';
    ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';
    /* Multi-layer glow */
    [[24,'rgba(201,168,76,0.15)'],[16,'rgba(201,168,76,0.25)'],[10,'rgba(201,168,76,0.45)'],[4,'rgba(255,215,0,0.7)']].forEach(([blur,col])=>{
      ctx.shadowColor=col;ctx.shadowBlur=blur*pulse;
      ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.fillText('\u03A9',CX,CY+fs*.06);
    });
    ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    /* Final crisp layer */
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,215,0,'+pulse+')';
    ctx.fillText('\u03A9',CX,CY+fs*.06);

    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }
  frame();

  /* Small nav logo */
  (function(){
    const ncv=document.getElementById('nav-omega');
    if(!ncv) return;
    const nctx=ncv.getContext('2d');let nt=0;
    function nf(){
      nt+=.03;nctx.clearRect(0,0,64,64);
      nctx.save();nctx.translate(32,32);
      [[28,.5],[22,-.8],[15,.9]].forEach(([r,sp],i)=>{
        nctx.save();nctx.rotate(nt*sp);
        nctx.beginPath();nctx.ellipse(0,0,r,r*Math.abs(Math.cos(nt*.2+i)),0,0,Math.PI*2);
        nctx.strokeStyle=['rgba(201,168,76,0.6)','rgba(0,229,255,0.4)','rgba(201,168,76,0.8)'][i];
        nctx.lineWidth=1;nctx.stroke();nctx.restore();
      });
      nctx.fillStyle='rgba(201,168,76,'+(0.8+.2*Math.sin(nt*2))+')';
      nctx.shadowColor='rgba(201,168,76,.7)';nctx.shadowBlur=8;
      nctx.font='700 18px "Cinzel Decorative",serif';nctx.textAlign='center';nctx.textBaseline='middle';
      nctx.fillText('\u03A9',0,1);nctx.shadowBlur=0;nctx.restore();
      requestAnimationFrame(nf);
    }
    nf();
  })();
})();

/* ===== AUTH STATE CTA ===== */
(async function(){

        function globalCommand(action) {
            console.log(`Executing global system operation: ${action}`);
            if (action === 'BACK') history.back();
            if (action === 'NEXT') alert('Awaiting next node sequencing data block.');
        }

        document.getElementById('omniSearch').addEventListener('keypress', function(e) {
            if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                alert(`Querying Omni-Fulfillment Network for: "${this.value}"`);
            }
        });

        // Matrix Background Visuals Generator
        const canvas = document.getElementById('matrixCanvas');
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        function resize() {
            canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
            canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
        }
        window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
        resize();

        const units = Array(Math.floor(canvas.width / 20)).fill(0);
        function draw() {
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(5, 5, 8, 0.05)';
            ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
            ctx.fillStyle = '#00ffcc';
            ctx.font = '14px monospace';
            units.forEach((y, index) => {
                const text = String.fromCharCode(Math.floor(Math.random() * 33) + 96);
                const x = index * 20;
                ctx.fillText(text, x, y);
                if (y > canvas.height && Math.random() > 0.975) {
                    units[index] = 0;
                } else {
                    units[index] = y + 20;
                }
            });
        }
        setInterval(draw, 50);
    </script>

function drawRadar(a,b,c){
  const cv=document.getElementById('radar-cv'); if(!cv)return;
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d');
  const CX=200,CY=200,RMAX=150,N=9;
  const auth=Math.sqrt(a*a+b*b+c*c);
  /* nine matrix dimensions derived from the three live axes */
  const vals=[a,b,c,(a+b+c)/3,(b+c)/2,(a+c)/2,(a+b)/2,(a+b+c)/3,auth/15.588*9];
  const cols=DIMENSIONS.map(d=>d.col);
  const TAG=['KNW','MST','CTR','RES','LIN','ECO','CEL','SOV','\u03A9'];
  let t=0;
  function frame(){
    t+=0.016; ctx.clearRect(0,0,400,400);
    const spin=t*0.14, sweep=(t*0.85)%(Math.PI*2), pulse=0.85+0.15*Math.sin(t*1.7);
    /* concentric grid rings; apex ring brighter */
    for(let r=1;r<=9;r++){
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(CX,CY,r/9*RMAX,0,Math.PI*2);
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(201,168,76,'+(r===9?0.30:0.06)+')'; ctx.lineWidth=r===9?1.2:0.5; ctx.stroke();
    }
    /* rotating outer tick ring */
    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(CX,CY); ctx.rotate(spin);
    for(let i=0;i<48;i++){const a2=i/48*Math.PI*2,r1=RMAX+5,r2=RMAX+(i%4===0?13:8);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(Math.cos(a2)*r1,Math.sin(a2)*r1); ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(a2)*r2,Math.sin(a2)*r2);
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(201,168,76,0.13)'; ctx.lineWidth=0.7; ctx.stroke();}
    ctx.restore();
    /* nine spokes */
    for(let i=0;i<N;i++){const ang=-Math.PI/2+i*(Math.PI*2/N);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(CX,CY); ctx.lineTo(CX+Math.cos(ang)*RMAX,CY+Math.sin(ang)*RMAX);
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(201,168,76,0.11)'; ctx.lineWidth=0.6; ctx.stroke();}
    /* sonar sweep wedge */
    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(CX,CY);
    for(let k=0;k<20;k++){const aa=sweep-k*0.045,al=0.16*(1-k/20);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0,0); ctx.arc(0,0,RMAX,aa,aa+0.05); ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,229,255,'+al.toFixed(3)+')'; ctx.fill();}
    ctx.restore();
    /* data hull across nine dimensions */
    const v=[];
    for(let i=0;i<N;i++){const ang=-Math.PI/2+i*(Math.PI*2/N);
      const rr=Math.max(0,Math.min(9,vals[i]))/9*RMAX*pulse;
      v.push([CX+Math.cos(ang)*rr,CY+Math.sin(ang)*rr]);}
    ctx.beginPath(); v.forEach((p,i)=>i?ctx.lineTo(p[0],p[1]):ctx.moveTo(p[0],p[1])); ctx.closePath();
    const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(CX,CY,0,CX,CY,RMAX);
    g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(201,168,76,0.34)'); g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(0,229,255,0.05)');
    ctx.fillStyle=g; ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(201,168,76,0.78)'; ctx.lineWidth=1.5;
    ctx.shadowColor='rgba(201,168,76,0.5)'; ctx.shadowBlur=10; ctx.stroke(); ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    /* vertex nodes, apex Omega larger */
    for(let i=0;i<N;i++){const om=(i===8);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.fillStyle=cols[i]; ctx.shadowColor=cols[i]; ctx.shadowBlur=om?15:7;
      ctx.arc(v[i][0],v[i][1],om?3.6:2.2,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();}
    ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    /* upright dimension tags */
    for(let i=0;i<N;i++){const ang=-Math.PI/2+i*(Math.PI*2/N);
      const lx=CX+Math.cos(ang)*(RMAX+24),ly=CY+Math.sin(ang)*(RMAX+24);
      ctx.fillStyle=cols[i]; ctx.font='700 11px "Courier Prime",monospace';
      ctx.textAlign='center'; ctx.textBaseline='middle'; ctx.fillText(TAG[i],lx,ly);}
    /* centre Omega core */
    ctx.font='700 22px "Cinzel Decorative",Georgia,serif'; ctx.textAlign='center'; ctx.textBaseline='middle';
    ctx.shadowColor='rgba(201,168,76,0.85)'; ctx.shadowBlur=14;
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(201,168,76,'+(0.7+0.3*Math.sin(t*1.4)).toFixed(2)+')';
    ctx.fillText('\u03A9',CX,CY); ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }
  frame();
}

function buildStream(a,b,c){
  const logs=[];
  const t=new Date();
  for(let i=0;i<24;i++){
    const types=['[INFO]','[SYNC]','[AUTH]','[EXEC]','[DATA]'];
    const msgs=['Matrix node event processed','Frequency lock maintained: 9.17Hz','Agent mesh handshake verified','Knowledge axis event +0.25','Contribution vector updated',`Authority score: ${Math.sqrt(a*a+b*b+c*c).toFixed(3)}`,'Supabase sync completed','Cache invalidated -- fresh data loaded','PQC handshake verified (FIPS 208)'];
    const type=types[i%5]; const msg=msgs[i%msgs.length];
    const h=(t.getHours()-Math.floor(i/4)).toString().padStart(2,'0');
    const m=(t.getMinutes()-i%4*15+60)%60;
    logs.push(`<div style="opacity:${1-i*.04}">${h}:${m.toString().padStart(2,'0')} <span style="color:var(--gold)">${type}</span> ${msg}</div>`);
  }
  // Duplicate for seamless loop
  const all=[...logs,...logs];
  const el=document.getElementById('stream-inner');
  if(el) el.innerHTML=all.join('');
}

function buildMetrics(a,b,c,tasks){
  const auth=Math.sqrt(a*a+b*b+c*c);
  const nodes=Math.round(a*b*c*9);
  const g=document.getElementById('metrics-row'); g.innerHTML='';
  [
    {l:'AUTHORITY',v:auth.toFixed(2),col:'#C9A84C',trend:'+0.25 today'},
    {l:'MATRIX NODES',v:nodes,col:'#00E5FF',trend:'Active'},
    {l:'KNOWLEDGE A',v:a.toFixed(1),col:'#00E5FF',trend:'Axis A'},
    {l:'MASTERY B',v:b.toFixed(1),col:'#E86A3A',trend:'Axis B'},
    {l:'CONTRIBUTION C',v:c.toFixed(1),col:'#3fb27f',trend:'Axis C'},
    {l:'TASKS COMPLETE',v:tasks,col:'#E2C86D',trend:'All time'},
  ].forEach(m=>{
    const card=document.createElement('div'); card.className='metric-card'; card.style.setProperty('--col',m.col);
    card.innerHTML=`<div class="mc-label">${m.l}</div><div class="mc-val" style="color:${m.col}">${m.v}</div><div class="mc-trend" style="color:var(--muted)">${m.trend}</div>`;
    g.appendChild(card);
  });
}

function buildAgentLog(){
  const g=document.getElementById('agent-log'); g.innerHTML='';
  const now=new Date();
  AGENTS_LOG.forEach((al,i)=>{
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='al-entry';
    const t=new Date(now-i*47000);
    const ts=t.getHours().toString().padStart(2,'0')+':'+t.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2,'0');
    d.innerHTML=`<div class="ale-time">${ts} UTC</div><div class="ale-dot" style="background:${al.col};color:${al.col}"></div><div class="ale-body"><div class="alb-agent" style="color:${al.col}">${al.name} AGENT</div><div class="alb-action">${al.action}</div></div>`;
    g.appendChild(d);
  });
}

function buildDimensions(a,b,c){
  const auth=Math.sqrt(a*a+b*b+c*c);
  const g=document.getElementById('dim-grid'); g.innerHTML='';
  DIMENSIONS.forEach((dim,i)=>{
    const vals=[a/9,b/9,c/9,auth/15.58,Math.random()*.5+.3,Math.random()*.4+.2,auth/20,auth/15,auth/15.58];
    const pct=Math.round(vals[i]*100);
    const c2=document.createElement('div'); c2.className='dim-card';
    c2.style.borderTopColor=dim.col+'55';
    c2.innerHTML=`<div class="dc-n" style="color:${dim.col}">${dim.n}</div><div class="dc-name" style="color:${dim.col}">${dim.name}</div><div class="dc-desc">${dim.desc}</div><div class="dc-bar-wrap"><div class="dc-bar" style="width:0%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,${dim.col},${dim.col}55)"></div></div><div class="dc-val" style="color:${dim.col}">${pct}% ACTIVE</div>`;
    g.appendChild(c2);
    setTimeout(()=>c2.querySelector('.dc-bar').style.width=pct+'%',200+i*80);
  });
}

async function boot(){
  const sess=(await sb.auth.getSession()).data.session;
  if(!sess){window.location.href='/account.html';return;}
  const pr=await sb.from('profiles').select('axis_a,axis_b,axis_c').eq('id',sess.user.id).maybeSingle();
  const a=parseFloat(pr.data?.axis_a)||1,b=parseFloat(pr.data?.axis_b)||1,c=parseFloat(pr.data?.axis_c)||1;
  const {count:tasks}=await sb.from('task_completions').select('*',{count:'exact',head:true}).eq('user_id',sess.user.id);
  buildMetrics(a,b,c,tasks||0);
  buildStream(a,b,c);
  buildAgentLog();
  buildDimensions(a,b,c);
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>drawRadar(a,b,c));
}
boot();
</script>

</body>
</html>

<script>
(function(){
  var cv=document.getElementById('bg-cv');
  var ctx=cv.getContext('2d');var t=0;
  var stars=Array.from({length:120},function(){return{x:Math.random(),y:Math.random(),r:Math.random()*1.4+.2,s:Math.random()*.4+.05};});
  function resize(){cv.width=window.innerWidth;cv.height=window.innerHeight;}
  resize();window.addEventListener('resize',resize);
  function f(){
    t+=.005;ctx.clearRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
    stars.forEach(function(s){
      var opa=0.04+0.14*Math.sin(t*s.s*3+s.x*6);
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(s.x*cv.width,s.y*cv.height,s.r*(0.6+0.4*Math.sin(t*s.s*2)),0,Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(201,168,76,'+opa+')';ctx.fill();
    });
    requestAnimationFrame(f);}f();
})();

/* Omega canvas */
(function(){
  var cv=document.getElementById('gate-omega');
  var ctx=cv.getContext('2d'),CX=80,CY=80;var t=0;
  function f(){
    t+=.02;ctx.clearRect(0,0,160,160);
    [[65,.4,1.5,'rgba(201,168,76,0.6)'],[48,-.6,1,'rgba(0,229,255,0.35)'],[32,.9,.8,'rgba(201,168,76,0.5)']].forEach(function(r){
      ctx.save();ctx.translate(CX,CY);ctx.rotate(t*r[1]);
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,r[0],r[0]*Math.abs(Math.cos(t*.15)),0,0,Math.PI*2);
      ctx.strokeStyle=r[3];ctx.lineWidth=r[2];ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();
    });
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(201,168,76,'+(0.82+.18*Math.sin(t*1.5))+')';
    ctx.shadowColor='rgba(201,168,76,.7)';ctx.shadowBlur=18+6*Math.sin(t);
    ctx.font='700 38px "Cinzel Decorative",serif';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';
    ctx.fillText('\u03A9',CX,CY+2);ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    requestAnimationFrame(f);}f();
})();

<script type="module">
import{createClient}from'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2';
const sb=createClient("https://ydqhzvvoyufiiqvzcjns.supabase.co","sb_publishable_9KlhhnvRs4OKgw6nxXHmYw_GxszJ46q");

async function checkStatus(){
  const sess=(await sb.auth.getSession()).data.session;
  if(!sess){window.location.href='/account.html';return;}
  const {data:pr}=await sb.from('profiles').select('is_owner,access_approved,is_trial,trial_expires_at').eq('id',sess.user.id).maybeSingle();
  if(!pr) return;
  /* Owner always gets through */
  if(pr.is_owner||pr.access_approved){window.location.href='/dashboard.html';return;}
  /* Active trial */
  if(pr.is_trial&&pr.trial_expires_at&&new Date(pr.trial_expires_at)>Date.now()){
    window.location.href='/dashboard.html';return;
  }
  document.getElementById('sb-status').textContent='STILL PENDING -- ARCHITECT WILL REVIEW SHORTLY';
}

/* Auto-check every 15 seconds */
checkStatus();
setInterval(checkStatus,15000);

document.getElementById('btn-check').addEventListener('click',checkStatus);
document.getElementById('btn-logout').addEventListener('click',async(e)=>{
  e.preventDefault();
  await sb.auth.signOut();
  window.location.href='/account.html';
});
</script>

<script>
(function(){
  var cv=document.getElementById('scene'), ctx=cv.getContext('2d');
  var W=0,H=0,DPR=Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio||1,2);
  var reduce = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var particles=[], t=0, raf=null;

  function resize(){
    W=window.innerWidth; H=window.innerHeight;
    cv.width=Math.floor(W*DPR); cv.height=Math.floor(H*DPR);
    cv.style.width=W+'px'; cv.style.height=H+'px';
    ctx.setTransform(DPR,0,0,DPR,0,0);
  }
  function init(){
    var n=Math.max(36, Math.min(96, Math.floor((W*H)/16000)));
    particles=[];
    for(var i=0;i<n;i++){
      particles.push({ x:Math.random()*W, y:Math.random()*H, vx:(Math.random()-0.5)*0.18, vy:(Math.random()-0.5)*0.18, r:Math.random()*1.6+0.6, gold:Math.random()<0.72 });
    }
  }
  function tri(cx,cy,R,rot,color){
    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(cx,cy); ctx.rotate(rot);
    ctx.strokeStyle=color; ctx.beginPath();
    for(var k=0;k<3;k++){ var a=-Math.PI/2+k*2*Math.PI/3; var x=Math.cos(a)*R, y=Math.sin(a)*R; if(k===0)ctx.moveTo(x,y); else ctx.lineTo(x,y); }
    ctx.closePath(); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore();
  }
  function sigil(cx,cy){
    var R=Math.min(W,H)*0.15;
    ctx.lineWidth=1;
    ctx.shadowColor='rgba(201,168,76,0.6)'; ctx.shadowBlur=16;
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(201,168,76,0.35)';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx,cy,R,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx,cy,R*0.66,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(201,168,76,0.16)'; ctx.stroke();
    tri(cx,cy,R*0.92,t,'rgba(232,106,58,0.85)');
    tri(cx,cy,R*0.92,-t*0.7+Math.PI,'rgba(0,229,255,0.5)');
    ctx.shadowColor='rgba(201,168,76,0.95)'; ctx.shadowBlur=26;
    ctx.fillStyle='#C9A84C'; ctx.textAlign='center'; ctx.textBaseline='middle';
    ctx.font='700 '+Math.round(R*0.62)+'px "Cinzel Decorative", serif';
    ctx.fillText('\u03A9', cx, cy+R*0.03);
    ctx.shadowBlur=0;
  }
  function draw(){
    ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
    var cx=W/2, cy=H*0.40;
    var g=ctx.createRadialGradient(cx,cy,20,cx,cy,Math.max(W,H)*0.62);
    g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(201,168,76,0.07)'); g.addColorStop(0.5,'rgba(0,229,255,0.025)'); g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(10,10,15,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle=g; ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
    var i,j;
    if(!reduce){ for(i=0;i<particles.length;i++){ var p=particles[i]; p.x+=p.vx; p.y+=p.vy; if(p.x<0)p.x+=W; if(p.x>W)p.x-=W; if(p.y<0)p.y+=H; if(p.y>H)p.y-=H; } }
    ctx.lineWidth=0.6;
    for(i=0;i<particles.length;i++){ for(j=i+1;j<particles.length;j++){ var a=particles[i],b=particles[j]; var dx=a.x-b.x,dy=a.y-b.y; var d2=dx*dx+dy*dy; if(d2<14000){ var al=(1-d2/14000)*0.22; ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(201,168,76,'+al.toFixed(3)+')'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(a.x,a.y); ctx.lineTo(b.x,b.y); ctx.stroke(); } } }
    for(i=0;i<particles.length;i++){ var q=particles[i]; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.fillStyle=q.gold?'rgba(201,168,76,0.9)':'rgba(0,229,255,0.8)'; ctx.shadowColor=q.gold?'rgba(201,168,76,0.8)':'rgba(0,229,255,0.7)'; ctx.shadowBlur=8; ctx.arc(q.x,q.y,q.r,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill(); }
    ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    sigil(cx,cy);
  }
  function loop(){ t+=0.004; draw(); raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop); }

  function start(){ resize(); init(); if(reduce){ draw(); } else { if(raf) cancelAnimationFrame(raf); loop(); } }
  window.addEventListener('resize', function(){ resize(); init(); if(reduce) draw(); });
  if(document.fonts && document.fonts.ready){ document.fonts.ready.then(start); } else { start(); }
})();
</script>

<script>
/* Tab switching */
document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(btn=>{
  btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-panel').forEach(p=>p.classList.remove('active'));
    btn.classList.add('active');
    document.getElementById('tab-'+btn.dataset.tab)?.classList.add('active');
  });
});

/* Hero particle canvas */
(function(){
  const cv=document.getElementById('ph-canvas');
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d');let t=0;
  function resize(){cv.width=cv.offsetWidth;cv.height=cv.offsetHeight||220;}
  resize();window.addEventListener('resize',resize);
  const pts=Array.from({length:60},()=>({x:Math.random(),y:Math.random(),r:Math.random()*1.5+.3,s:Math.random()*.4+.05}));
  function f(){
    t+=.005;ctx.clearRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
    /* Gradient overlay */
    const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,cv.width,cv.height*.7);
    g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(7,7,11,0.7)');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(201,168,76,0.04)');
    ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
    /* Stars */
    pts.forEach(p=>{
      const opa=0.06+0.18*Math.sin(t*p.s*3+p.x*6);
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x*cv.width,p.y*cv.height,p.r,0,Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(201,168,76,'+opa+')';ctx.fill();
    });
    requestAnimationFrame(f);}f();
})();

/* Avatar canvas */
(function(){
  const cv=document.getElementById('av-canvas');
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d'),CX=100,CY=100;let t=0;
  function f(){
    t+=.018;ctx.clearRect(0,0,200,200);
    const bg=ctx.createRadialGradient(CX,CY,0,CX,CY,92);
    bg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(201,168,76,0.4)');bg.addColorStop(.5,'rgba(201,168,76,0.12)');bg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(7,7,11,0.9)');
    ctx.fillStyle=bg;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(CX,CY,94,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
    /* 3 rotating rings */
    [[76,.4,1.5],[56,-.65,1],[38,.9,.8]].forEach(([r,sp,lw],i)=>{
      ctx.save();ctx.translate(CX,CY);ctx.rotate(t*sp);
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,r,r*Math.abs(Math.cos(t*.2+i)),0,0,Math.PI*2);
      ctx.strokeStyle=['rgba(201,168,76,0.7)','rgba(0,229,255,0.4)','rgba(201,168,76,0.5)'][i];
      ctx.lineWidth=lw;ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();
    });
    /* Omega */
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(201,168,76,'+(0.88+.12*Math.sin(t*1.5))+')';
    ctx.shadowColor='rgba(201,168,76,.8)';ctx.shadowBlur=20+6*Math.sin(t);
    ctx.font='900 38px "Cinzel Decorative",serif';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';
    ctx.fillText('\u03A9',CX,CY+2);ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    requestAnimationFrame(f);}f();
})();

function drawAchBadge(cv,sym,col){
  cv.width=80;cv.height=80;cv.style.width='40px';cv.style.height='40px';
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d');let t=0;
  function f(){
    t+=.03;ctx.clearRect(0,0,80,80);
    const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(40,40,0,40,40,36);g.addColorStop(0,col+'44');g.addColorStop(1,'transparent');
    ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(40,40,36,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(40,40);ctx.rotate(t*.5);
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,32,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.strokeStyle=col+'55';ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();
    ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.shadowColor=col;ctx.shadowBlur=8;
    ctx.font='700 16px "Courier Prime",monospace';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';
    ctx.fillText(sym,40,41);ctx.shadowBlur=0;requestAnimationFrame(f);}f();
}

function buildAchievements(){
  const g=document.getElementById('ach-grid');g.innerHTML='';
  ACHIEV.forEach(a=>{
    const card=document.createElement('div');card.className='ach-badge';
    card.style.cssText=`border-color:${a.col}55;border-top:2px solid ${a.col}88`;
    const cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.className='ab-canvas';
    card.innerHTML=`<div class="ab-name" style="color:${a.col}">${a.name}</div><span class="ab-tier" style="color:${a.col};border-color:${a.col}44">${a.type||'TROPHY'}</span>`;
    card.insertBefore(cv,card.firstChild);
    g.appendChild(card);
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>drawAchBadge(cv,a.sym,a.col));
  });
}

function buildTiers(){
  const g=document.getElementById('tiers-display');
  g.innerHTML=MATS.map(m=>`<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;padding:12px 0;border-bottom:1px dashed rgba(201,168,76,.06)"><div style="width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:50%;border:2px solid ${m.col};display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-family:var(--D);font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:${m.col};box-shadow:0 0 12px ${m.col}55;flex-shrink:0">${m.n}</div><div style="flex:1"><div style="font-family:var(--D);font-size:13px;color:${m.col};font-weight:700;margin-bottom:2px">${m.name}</div><div style="font-family:var(--M);font-size:8px;color:var(--muted)">THRESHOLD: ${m.thresh}</div></div><span style="font-family:var(--M);font-size:8px;letter-spacing:2px;padding:3px 8px;border:1px solid ${m.col}55;color:${m.col}">SEALED &#x2713;</span></div>`).join('');
}

function buildPhases(){
  const g=document.getElementById('phases-display');
  g.innerHTML=PHASES.map((p,i)=>`<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px dashed rgba(201,168,76,.06)"><div style="width:28px;height:28px;border-radius:50%;border:2px solid rgba(201,168,76,.6);background:rgba(201,168,76,.12);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-family:var(--M);font-size:10px;color:var(--gold);flex-shrink:0">&#x2713;</div><div style="font-family:var(--D);font-size:12px;color:var(--solar);font-weight:700;flex:1">${p.n}. ${p.name}</div><span style="font-family:var(--M);font-size:7px;letter-spacing:2px;color:var(--green)" data-i18n="status.complete">COMPLETE</span></div>`).join('');
}

function buildConsole(){
  const g=document.getElementById('console-grid');
  const btns=[
    {g:'\u25CF',t:'ACCESS CONTROL',d:'Grant or revoke member access. Live trial timers.',href:'/approvals.html'},
    {g:'\u2609',t:'ALL MEMBERS',d:'Full member registry with identity and axis data.',href:'/approvals.html'},
    {g:'\u25C8',t:'COMPLIANCE',d:'Platform governance, charter, and legal records.',href:'/compliance.html'},
    {g:'\u2736',t:'INTELLIGENCE',d:'Full analytics, AI agent logs, dimension viewer.',href:'/intelligence.html'},
    {g:'\u22D4',t:'BLOODLINE VAULT',d:'Succession, inheritance, and heir protocols.',href:'/bloodline.html'},

  ];
  g.innerHTML='';
  btns.forEach(b=>{

    el.innerHTML=`<span class="cb-glyph">${b.g}</span><div class="cb-title">${b.t}</div><div class="cb-desc">${b.d}</div>`;
    el.addEventListener('click',()=>window.location.href=b.href);

  });
}

async function boot(){
  const sess=(await sb.auth.getSession()).data.session;
  if(!sess){window.location.href='/account.html';return;}
  /* Animate axis bars */
  setTimeout(()=>{
    document.getElementById('bar-a').style.width='100%';
    document.getElementById('bar-b').style.width='100%';
    document.getElementById('bar-c').style.width='100%';
  },400);
  const pr=await sb.from('profiles').select('is_owner,display_name').eq('id',sess.user.id).maybeSingle();
  const isOwner=pr.data?.is_owner||false;
  if(isOwner){
    document.getElementById('arch-console').style.display='block';
    buildConsole();
    /* SHOW ACCESS CONTROL TAB */
    const atb=document.getElementById('access-tab-btn');
    if(atb) atb.style.display='flex';
    window.__ownerSB=sb;
    loadMembers();
    /* Auto-reload pending count every 30s */
    setInterval(loadMembers, 30000);
  }
  buildAchievements();buildTiers();buildPhases();buildCerts();
}
boot();

<script>

/* =====================================================
   ACCESS CONTROL SYSTEM  --  OWNER ONLY
   ===================================================== */
window.__selectedMinutes = 9.1717; /* FIXED -- never changes */
window.__allMembers = [];
window.__timers = {};
window.__activeFilter = 'pending';

/* Period selector removed -- FIXED at 9.1717 minutes */

function startCountdown(uid,expiresAt,el){
  if(window.__timers[uid]) clearInterval(window.__timers[uid]);
  function tick(){
    const diff=new Date(expiresAt)-Date.now();
    if(diff<=0){el.textContent='EXPIRED';clearInterval(window.__timers[uid]);loadMembers();return;}
    const m=Math.floor(diff/60000),s=Math.floor((diff%60000)/1000);
    el.textContent=m.toString().padStart(2,'0')+':'+s.toString().padStart(2,'0');
  }
  tick();window.__timers[uid]=setInterval(tick,1000);
}

async function grantAccess(uid,minutes,sbRef){
  // Owner-gated RPCs are the source of truth. The trial is a fixed 9.1717 min
  // (approve_member); negative minutes means permanent (grant_permanent_access).
  try{
    if(minutes<0){ const{error}=await sbRef.rpc('grant_permanent_access',{p_uid:uid}); if(error) throw error; }
    else { const{error}=await sbRef.rpc('approve_member',{p_uid:uid}); if(error) throw error; }
  }catch(e){
    // fallback: direct write, still the canonical 9.1717 minutes
    if(minutes<0){ await sbRef.from('profiles').update({access_approved:true,is_trial:false,trial_expires_at:null}).eq('id',uid); }
    else { const exp=new Date(Date.now()+9.1717*60000).toISOString(); await sbRef.from('profiles').update({access_approved:true,is_trial:true,trial_expires_at:exp}).eq('id',uid); }
  }
  loadMembers();
}

async function revokeAccess(uid,sbRef){
  try{ const{error}=await sbRef.rpc('revoke_member',{p_uid:uid}); if(error) throw error; }
  catch(e){ await sbRef.from('profiles').update({access_approved:false,is_trial:false,trial_expires_at:null}).eq('id',uid); }
  loadMembers();
}

function buildMemberList(members){
  const filter=window.__activeFilter;
  let rows=members;
  if(filter==='pending') rows=members.filter(m=>!m.is_owner&&!m.access_approved);
  else if(filter==='trial') rows=members.filter(m=>m.is_trial&&m.trial_expires_at&&new Date(m.trial_expires_at)>Date.now());
  else if(filter==='approved') rows=members.filter(m=>m.access_approved&&!m.is_trial&&!m.is_owner);

  const g=document.getElementById('mbr-list');
  if(!g) return;

  /* Update pending alert */
  const pendingCount=members.filter(m=>!m.is_owner&&!m.access_approved).length;
  const alertEl=document.getElementById('ac-alert');
  const countEl=document.getElementById('aca-count');
  const badge=document.getElementById('access-badge');
  if(alertEl) alertEl.style.display=pendingCount>0?'block':'none';
  if(countEl) countEl.textContent=pendingCount;
  if(badge){badge.textContent=pendingCount;badge.style.display=pendingCount>0?'flex':'none';}

  /* Skip owner in list */
  const display=rows.filter(m=>!m.is_owner);
  if(!display.length){
    g.innerHTML='<div style="padding:24px clamp(14px,3vw,36px);font-family:var(--M);font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);letter-spacing:2px">NO MEMBERS IN THIS CATEGORY</div>';
    return;
  }
  g.innerHTML='';
  display.forEach(m=>{
    const isPending=!m.access_approved;
    const isTrial=m.is_trial&&m.trial_expires_at&&new Date(m.trial_expires_at)>Date.now();
    const isApproved=m.access_approved&&!m.is_trial;
    const col=isApproved?'var(--green)':isTrial?'var(--gold)':isPending?'var(--crim)':'var(--muted)';
    const statusLabel=isApproved?'APPROVED':isTrial?'TRIAL ACTIVE':isPending?'PENDING':'EXPIRED';
    const init=(m.display_name||m.email||'M')[0].toUpperCase();

    const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='mbr-row';
    row.innerHTML=
      '<div class="mbr-init" style="border-color:'+col+'44;color:'+col+'">'+init+'</div>'+
      '<div class="mbr-meta">'+
        '<div class="mbr-email">'+(m.display_name||'(no name)')+'</div>'+
        '<div class="mbr-detail">'+(m.email||m.id||'').slice(0,32)+' . Joined: '+(m.created_at?new Date(m.created_at).toLocaleDateString():' -- ')+'</div>'+
      '</div>'+
      '<div class="mbr-status">'+
        '<span class="mbr-pill" style="color:'+col+';border-color:'+col+'44">'+statusLabel+'</span>'+
        (isTrial?'<span class="mbr-timer" id="tmr-'+m.id+'">--:--</span>':'')+
      '</div>'+
      '<div class="mbr-actions">'+
        (isPending||isTrial?'<button class="mbr-btn btn-gtrial" data-uid="'+m.id+'">GRANT 9.1717 MIN</button>':'')+
        (!isApproved?'<button class="mbr-btn btn-gperm" data-uid="'+m.id+'">GRANT PERM</button>':'')+
        (isTrial?'<button class="mbr-btn btn-extend" data-uid="'+m.id+'" data-i18n="prof.extend">EXTEND</button>':'')+
        ((isApproved||isTrial)?'<button class="mbr-btn btn-revoke" data-uid="'+m.id+'">REVOKE</button>':'')+
      '</div>';

    g.appendChild(row);

    if(isTrial){const tel=row.querySelector('#tmr-'+m.id);if(tel) startCountdown(m.id,m.trial_expires_at,tel);}
  });
}

async function loadMembers(){
  if(!window.__ownerSB) return;
  const sb=window.__ownerSB;
  let members=[];
  try{
    const {data:rpc}=await sb.rpc('get_all_members');
    members=rpc||[];
  }catch(e){
    const {data:fb}=await sb.from('profiles').select('*').order('created_at',{ascending:false});
    members=fb||[];
  }
  window.__allMembers=members;
  buildMemberList(members);
  /* Attach button handlers after render */
  document.querySelectorAll('.btn-gtrial,.btn-extend').forEach(btn=>{
    btn.addEventListener('click',e=>grantAccess(e.target.dataset.uid,window.__selectedMinutes,sb));
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.btn-gperm').forEach(btn=>{
    btn.addEventListener('click',e=>grantAccess(e.target.dataset.uid,-1,sb));
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.btn-revoke').forEach(btn=>{
    btn.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(confirm('Revoke access?')) revokeAccess(e.target.dataset.uid,sb);});
  });
}

/* Filter buttons */
document.querySelectorAll('.acf-btn').forEach(btn=>{
  btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
    document.querySelectorAll('.acf-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('acf-on'));
    btn.classList.add('acf-on');
    window.__activeFilter=btn.dataset.filter;
    buildMemberList(window.__allMembers);
  });
});

</script>

<script type="module">
  import { createClient } from 'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2';
  const supabase = createClient("https://ydqhzvvoyufiiqvzcjns.supabase.co","sb_publishable_9KlhhnvRs4OKgw6nxXHmYw_GxszJ46q");

  function show(which){
    document.getElementById('view-request').classList.toggle('hidden', which!=='request');
    document.getElementById('view-reset').classList.toggle('hidden', which!=='reset');
    document.getElementById('title').textContent = which==='reset' ? 'SET NEW PASSWORD' : 'PASSWORD RECOVERY';
  }

  var inRecovery = false;
  supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange(function(event){
    if(event==='PASSWORD_RECOVERY'){ inRecovery=true; show('reset'); }
  });
  // also detect from the link hash directly
  if(location.hash.indexOf('type=recovery')!==-1){ inRecovery=true; show('reset'); } else { show('request'); }

  async function sendLink(){
    var email=document.getElementById('r-email').value.trim();
    var msg=document.getElementById('r-msg'), btn=document.getElementById('r-send');
    if(!email || email.indexOf('@')<1){ msg.className='msg bad'; msg.textContent='Enter a valid email.'; return; }
    btn.disabled=true; msg.className='msg info'; msg.textContent='Sending...';
    var redirectTo = window.location.origin + '/reset.html';
    var r = await supabase.auth.resetPasswordForEmail(email, { redirectTo: redirectTo });
    btn.disabled=false;
    if(r.error){ msg.className='msg bad'; msg.textContent=r.error.message||'Could not send link.'; return; }
    msg.className='msg ok'; msg.textContent='If that email has an account, a recovery link is on its way. Check your inbox.';
  }

  async function updatePw(){
    var pw=document.getElementById('r-pw').value, pw2=document.getElementById('r-pw2').value;
    var msg=document.getElementById('r-msg2'), btn=document.getElementById('r-update');
    if(pw.length<6){ msg.className='msg bad'; msg.textContent='Password must be at least 6 characters.'; return; }
    if(pw!==pw2){ msg.className='msg bad'; msg.textContent='Passwords do not match.'; return; }
    btn.disabled=true; msg.className='msg info'; msg.textContent='Updating...';
    var r = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ password: pw });
    btn.disabled=false;
    if(r.error){ msg.className='msg bad'; msg.textContent=r.error.message||'Could not update. Request a fresh link.'; return; }
    msg.className='msg ok'; msg.textContent='Password updated. Entering the Order...';
    setTimeout(function(){ window.location.href='/dashboard.html'; }, 1400);
  }

  document.getElementById('r-send').addEventListener('click',sendLink);
  document.getElementById('r-update').addEventListener('click',updatePw);
  document.getElementById('r-email').addEventListener('keydown',function(e){ if(e.key==='Enter') sendLink(); });
  document.getElementById('r-pw2').addEventListener('keydown',function(e){ if(e.key==='Enter') updatePw(); });
</script>

<script type="module">
import { createClient } from 'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2';
const SB_URL='https://ydqhzvvoyufiiqvzcjns.supabase.co';
const SB_KEY='sb_publishable_9KlhhnvRs4OKgw6nxXHmYw_GxszJ46q';
const CHECKOUT=SB_URL+'/functions/v1/checkout';
const sb=createClient(SB_URL,SB_KEY);

const TIERS=[
  {mat:'SAND',        name:'Initiate',      price:'9.17',   color:'#c2b280', perks:['Matrix access, axes 0.001 to 9.000','Personal cosmology and agent','Academy tier one']},
  {mat:'GLASS',       name:'Seeker',        price:'19.17',  color:'#9fd3d8', perks:['Everything in Sand','Full academy path','Gaming arena entry']},
  {mat:'IRON',        name:'Adept',         price:'37.17',  color:'#9aa0a6', perks:['Everything in Glass','Contribution ledger','Certificates of ascent']},
  {mat:'STEEL',       name:'Warden',        price:'71.17',  color:'#b8c0c8', perks:['Everything in Iron','Family lineage system','Priority agent counsel']},
  {mat:'TITANIUM',    name:'Vanguard',      price:'137.17', color:'#c8cdd2', perks:['Everything in Steel','Media universe early access','Advanced analytics']},
  {mat:'CARBON',      name:'Architect',     price:'237.17', color:'#6f7378', perks:['Everything in Titanium','Consultancy marketplace','Heritage archives']},
  {mat:'GOLD',        name:'Sovereign',     price:'437.17', color:'#C9A84C', perks:['Everything in Carbon','Treasury and vault access','Featured on the Hall of Fame']},
  {mat:'PLATINUM',    name:'Luminary',      price:'637.17', color:'#dfe4e6', perks:['Everything in Gold','All twelve agents unbound','Private counsel channel']},
  {mat:'OMEGA MASTER',name:'Ascendant',     price:'917.17', color:'#E2C86D', perks:['Everything in Platinum','Apex-tier standing','The full sovereign ecosystem']},
];

let PAY_ON=false, CURRENT='none';

async function boot(){
  const { data:{ session } } = await sb.auth.getSession();
  let sub={tier:'none',status:'none',payments_enabled:false};
  if(session){ try{ const {data}=await sb.rpc('my_subscription'); if(data) sub=data; }catch(e){} }
  PAY_ON = sub.payments_enabled===true;
  CURRENT = (sub.tier||'none');
  const st=document.getElementById('sub-status');
  if(!PAY_ON){
    st.className='status dormant';
    st.innerHTML='<b>SUBSCRIPTIONS ARE BEING FINALIZED.</b> &nbsp;The nine tiers below are open for preview. Enrollment activates once the Order completes its final review.';
  } else if(sub.status==='active'||sub.status==='trialing'){
    st.className='status';
    st.innerHTML='CURRENT STANDING &nbsp;&#183;&nbsp; <b>'+String(sub.tier).toUpperCase()+'</b> &nbsp;&#183;&nbsp; '+String(sub.status).toUpperCase();
  } else {
    st.className='status';
    st.innerHTML='CHOOSE YOUR TIER TO BEGIN YOUR ASCENT.';
  }
  render();
  const q=new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('checkout');
  if(q==='success') setMsg('Enrollment received. Your standing will update shortly.','good');
  if(q==='cancel') setMsg('Enrollment canceled. No charge was made.','bad');
}

function render(){
  const g=document.getElementById('tier-grid'); g.innerHTML='';
  TIERS.forEach(t=>{
    const isCur = CURRENT && CURRENT.toUpperCase()===t.mat;
    const card=document.createElement('div');
    card.className='tier'+(isCur?' current':'');
    card.style.setProperty('--tc',t.color);
    let btn;
    if(isCur){ btn='<button class="btn" disabled>CURRENT TIER</button>'; }
    else if(!PAY_ON){ btn='<button class="btn" disabled>ACTIVATION PENDING</button>'; }
    else { btn='<button class="btn" data-tier="'+t.mat+'">ENROLL</button>'; }
    card.innerHTML=(isCur?'<span class="badge">ACTIVE</span>':'')
      +'<div class="mat">'+t.mat+'</div>'
      +'<h3>'+t.name+'</h3>'
      +'<div class="price">$'+t.price+'<span> / month</span></div>'
      +'<ul>'+t.perks.map(p=>'<li>'+p+'</li>').join('')+'</ul>'
      +btn;
    g.appendChild(card);
  });
  g.querySelectorAll('button[data-tier]').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>enroll(b.getAttribute('data-tier'),b)));
}

async function enroll(tier,btn){
  setMsg('',''); btn.disabled=true; const old=btn.textContent; btn.textContent='OPENING...';
  try{
    const { data:{ session } } = await sb.auth.getSession();
    if(!session){ location.href='/account.html'; return; }
    const r=await fetch(CHECKOUT,{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':'Bearer '+session.access_token},body:JSON.stringify({tier})});
    const d=await r.json();
    if(d.url){ location.href=d.url; return; }
    setMsg(d.message||'Enrollment is not available yet.','bad');
  }catch(e){ setMsg('Could not open enrollment. Please try again.','bad'); }
  btn.disabled=false; btn.textContent=old;
}

function setMsg(t,k){ const m=document.getElementById('sub-msg'); m.textContent=t; m.className='msg'+(k?(' '+k):''); }
boot();
</script>
<script src="/nav.js" defer></script>
<script src="/bg.js" defer></script>
</body>

function buildAssets(){
  function row(a){
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='asset-row';
    d.innerHTML=`<div class="ar-icon" style="color:${a.col}">${a.icon}</div><div class="ar-meta"><div class="ar-name">${a.name}</div><div class="ar-sub">${a.sub}</div></div><div style="text-align:right"><div class="ar-val">${a.val}</div><div class="ar-change" style="color:${a.change.startsWith('+')?' var(--green)':'var(--crim)'}">${a.change}</div></div>`;
    return d;
  }
  const pa=document.getElementById('physical-assets');
  PHYSICAL.forEach(a=>pa.appendChild(row(a)));
  const da=document.getElementById('digital-assets');
  DIGITAL.forEach(a=>da.appendChild(row(a)));
}

function buildNodes(){
  const g=document.getElementById('node-grid'); g.innerHTML='';
  NODES.forEach(n=>{
    const c=document.createElement('div'); c.className='node-cell';
    c.innerHTML=`<div class="nc-indicator" style="background:${n.col};box-shadow:0 0 8px ${n.col}"></div><div class="nc-body"><div class="ncb-label">${n.label}</div><div class="ncb-val" style="color:${n.col}">${n.val}</div></div>`;
    g.appendChild(c);
  });
}

function buildSplinter(){
  const g=document.getElementById('splinter-grid'); g.innerHTML='';
  INVESTMENTS.forEach(inv=>{
    const c=document.createElement('div'); c.className='splinter-card';
    c.style.setProperty('--col',inv.col);
    c.style.cssText+=`border-left:3px solid ${inv.col}88`;
    c.innerHTML=`<div class="sc-label">${inv.label}</div><div class="sc-pct" style="color:${inv.col}">${inv.pct}%</div><div class="sc-flow">${inv.flow}</div><div class="sc-bar-wrap"><div class="sc-bar-fill" style="width:0%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,${inv.col},transparent)"></div></div>`;
    g.appendChild(c);
    setTimeout(()=>c.querySelector('.sc-bar-fill').style.width=inv.pct+'%',300);
  });
}

/* RESONANCE WAVEFORM */
function drawWave(){
  const cv=document.getElementById('wave-cv');
  const W=cv.offsetWidth||200, H=40; cv.width=W*2; cv.height=H*2;
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d'); ctx.scale(2,2);
  let t=0;
  function ecgY(x,W,t){
    const cycle=x/W*3+t*.7, pos=cycle%1;
    if(pos<.1) return -Math.sin(pos/.1*Math.PI)*10;
    if(pos<.12) return Math.sin((pos-.1)/.02*Math.PI*2)*25;
    if(pos<.16) return -Math.sin((pos-.12)/.04*Math.PI)*12;
    if(pos<.2) return Math.sin((pos-.16)/.04*Math.PI)*5;
    return Math.sin(cycle*6.28*2)*1.5;
  }
  function frame(){
    t+=.015; ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
    ctx.beginPath();
    for(let x=0;x<W;x+=1.5){const y=H/2+ecgY(x,W,t);x===0?ctx.moveTo(x,y):ctx.lineTo(x,y);}
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(63,178,127,0.7)'; ctx.lineWidth=1.5;
    ctx.shadowColor='rgba(63,178,127,0.5)'; ctx.shadowBlur=6; ctx.stroke(); ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }
  frame();
}

/* 3D ROTATING VAULT CUBE */
function drawVaultCube(){
  const cv=document.getElementById('vault-cv');
  const S=140, ctx=cv.getContext('2d');
  let t=0;
  const cx=140,cy=140;

  function project(x,y,z,rx,ry){
    const cosX=Math.cos(rx),sinX=Math.sin(rx),cosY=Math.cos(ry),sinY=Math.sin(ry);
    let tx=x*cosY+z*sinY, tz=-x*sinY+z*cosY;
    let ty=y*cosX-tz*sinX, tz2=y*sinX+tz*cosX;
    const scale=S/(S+tz2*.8);
    return {x:cx+tx*scale,y:cy+ty*scale,scale};
  }

  function drawEdge(p1,p2,alpha,col){
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(p1.x,p1.y); ctx.lineTo(p2.x,p2.y);
    ctx.strokeStyle=col||`rgba(201,168,76,${alpha})`; ctx.lineWidth=1.5*Math.min(p1.scale,p2.scale);
    ctx.shadowColor=col||'rgba(201,168,76,0.6)'; ctx.shadowBlur=alpha*8;
    ctx.stroke(); ctx.shadowBlur=0;
  }

  const sz=50;
  const verts=[
    [-sz,-sz,-sz],[sz,-sz,-sz],[sz,sz,-sz],[-sz,sz,-sz],
    [-sz,-sz,sz],[sz,-sz,sz],[sz,sz,sz],[-sz,sz,sz]
  ];
  const edges=[[0,1],[1,2],[2,3],[3,0],[4,5],[5,6],[6,7],[7,4],[0,4],[1,5],[2,6],[3,7]];

  function frame(){
    t+=.008; ctx.clearRect(0,0,cx*2,cy*2);
    const rx=t*.4, ry=t*.6;
    const pts=verts.map(v=>project(v[0],v[1],v[2],rx,ry));
    /* Face fills */
    const faces=[[0,1,2,3],[4,5,6,7],[0,1,5,4],[2,3,7,6],[1,2,6,5],[0,3,7,4]];
    faces.forEach(f=>{
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(pts[f[0]].x,pts[f[0]].y);
      for(let i=1;i<4;i++) ctx.lineTo(pts[f[i]].x,pts[f[i]].y);
      ctx.closePath();
      const avgZ=(verts[f[0]][2]+verts[f[1]][2]+verts[f[2]][2]+verts[f[3]][2])/4;
      const alpha=.06+.04*Math.sin(t+avgZ*.01);
      ctx.fillStyle=`rgba(201,168,76,${alpha})`; ctx.fill();
    });
    /* Edges */
    edges.forEach(e=>drawEdge(pts[e[0]],pts[e[1]],.6));
    /* Corner dots */
    pts.forEach(p=>{
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,2,0,Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(201,168,76,0.8)'; ctx.shadowColor='rgba(201,168,76,0.7)'; ctx.shadowBlur=8; ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    });
    /* Center &#937; */
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(201,168,76,'+(0.7+0.25*Math.sin(t*1.5))+')';
    ctx.font='700 28px "Cinzel Decorative",serif'; ctx.textAlign='center'; ctx.textBaseline='middle';
    ctx.shadowColor='rgba(201,168,76,0.8)'; ctx.shadowBlur=20; ctx.fillText('\u03A9',cx,cy); ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }
  frame();
}

async function boot(){
  const sess=(await sb.auth.getSession()).data.session;
  if(!sess){window.location.href='/account.html';return;}
  const pr=await sb.from('profiles').select('axis_a,axis_b,axis_c').eq('id',sess.user.id).maybeSingle();
  const a=parseFloat(pr.data?.axis_a)||1,b=parseFloat(pr.data?.axis_b)||1,c=parseFloat(pr.data?.axis_c)||1;
  const nodes=Math.round(a*b*c*9);
  const xp=Math.round(nodes*917.17);
  document.getElementById('aum-val').innerHTML='&#937; '+xp.toLocaleString();
  document.getElementById('res-credits').textContent='\u03A9 '+Math.round(nodes*91.7).toLocaleString();
  document.getElementById('res-digital').textContent='\u03A9 '+Math.round(nodes*9.17).toLocaleString();
  buildAssets(); buildNodes(); buildSplinter();
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{drawWave();drawVaultCube();});
}
boot();

function drawFranchiseGlyph(cv,col){
  cv.width=120;cv.height=80;cv.style.width='60px';cv.style.height='40px';
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d');let t=0;
  function f(){
    t+=.025;ctx.clearRect(0,0,120,80);
    const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(60,40,0,60,40,50);g.addColorStop(0,col+'33');g.addColorStop(1,'transparent');
    ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(60,40,50,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(60,40);ctx.rotate(t*.4);
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,42,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.strokeStyle=col+'44';ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.shadowColor=col;ctx.shadowBlur=12;
    ctx.font='700 20px "Cinzel Decorative",serif';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';ctx.fillText('\u03A9',60,41);ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    requestAnimationFrame(f);
  }
  f();
}

    c.insertBefore(cv,c.firstChild);
    g.appendChild(c);
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>drawFranchiseGlyph(cv,fr.col));
  });
}

function buildGamesGrid(){

  GAMES.forEach((game,gi)=>{
    for(let si=1;si<=12;si++){
      const c=document.createElement('div');c.className='sg-card';
      const col=['#E86A3A','#C9A84C','#3fb27f','#00E5FF','#9B6BF0','#8B0000','#D9B86A','#C7CDD6','#E2C86D','#C9A84C','#9B6BF0','#C9A84C'][gi];
      c.style.borderLeft='3px solid '+col+'44';
      c.innerHTML=`<div class="sg-num" style="color:${col}">${gi+1}.${si}</div><div class="sg-title">${game.replace('&#x03A9;','&#937;')}: WORLD ${si}</div><div class="sg-film" style="color:${col}88">SUB-GAME ${si} OF 12</div>`;
      g.appendChild(c);
    }
  });
}

function buildChronos(){
  const g=document.getElementById('chronos-content');
  g.innerHTML=['BASE CARBON','ETHER BOUND','PMATE BOUND','OMEGA ABSOLUTE','NOURE HERITAGES','CORE OMEGA MASTER'].map((stage,i)=>`<div style="border:1px solid var(--line);background:linear-gradient(180deg,var(--panel),transparent);padding:16px;display:flex;gap:16px;align-items:center;margin-bottom:10px"><div style="font-family:var(--D);font-size:22px;color:var(--gold);min-width:32px">${i+1}</div><div><div style="font-family:var(--D);font-size:14px;color:var(--gold);margin-bottom:3px">${stage}</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:300">Ascension stage ${i+1} of 6 in the Chronos Omega Universe cycle.</div></div></div>`).join('');
}

function buildLebanon(){
  const g=document.getElementById('lebanon-content');
  g.innerHTML=['THE CEDAR ROOT','THE GLASS HORIZON','OMEGA HERITAGE','SYD OMEGA DEMOS','CULTURAL LEDGER'].map((ch,i)=>`<div style="border:1px solid var(--line);background:linear-gradient(180deg,var(--panel),transparent);padding:16px;display:flex;gap:16px;align-items:center;margin-bottom:10px"><div style="font-family:var(--D);font-size:22px;color:var(--solar);min-width:32px">${i+1}</div><div><div style="font-family:var(--D);font-size:14px;color:var(--solar);margin-bottom:3px">${ch}</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:300">Chapter ${i+1} of the Infinite Lebanon Saga -- Cedar Root Chronicles.</div></div></div>`).join('');
}

async function boot(){
  const sess=(await sb.auth.getSession()).data.session;
  if(!sess){window.location.href='/account.html';return;}
  buildFranchises();buildSeriesGrid();buildGamesGrid();buildChronos();buildLebanon();
}
boot();

<script>
/* Tab switching */
document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(btn=>{
  btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-panel').forEach(p=>p.classList.remove('active'));
    btn.classList.add('active');
    document.getElementById('tab-'+btn.dataset.tab)?.classList.add('active');
  });
});
if(location.hash){const t=location.hash.replace('#','');document.querySelector('[data-tab="'+t+'"]')?.click();}
</script>

function drawVaultCube(){
  const cv=document.getElementById('vault-cv');
  if(!cv) return;
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d'),CX=120,CY=120,sz=46; let t=0;
  const verts=[[-sz,-sz,-sz],[sz,-sz,-sz],[sz,sz,-sz],[-sz,sz,-sz],[-sz,-sz,sz],[sz,-sz,sz],[sz,sz,sz],[-sz,sz,sz]];
  const edges=[[0,1],[1,2],[2,3],[3,0],[4,5],[5,6],[6,7],[7,4],[0,4],[1,5],[2,6],[3,7]];
  function proj(x,y,z,rx,ry){
    const cX=Math.cos(rx),sX=Math.sin(rx),cY=Math.cos(ry),sY=Math.sin(ry);
    let tx=x*cY+z*sY,tz=-x*sY+z*cY;
    let ty=y*cX-tz*sX,tz2=y*sX+tz*cX;
    const sc=120/(120+tz2*.8); return{x:CX+tx*sc,y:CY+ty*sc};
  }
  function frame(){
    t+=.01; ctx.clearRect(0,0,240,240);
    const rx=t*.4,ry=t*.6,pts=verts.map(v=>proj(v[0],v[1],v[2],rx,ry));
    edges.forEach(e=>{
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(pts[e[0]].x,pts[e[0]].y);ctx.lineTo(pts[e[1]].x,pts[e[1]].y);
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(201,168,76,0.6)';ctx.lineWidth=1.5;ctx.shadowColor='rgba(201,168,76,.4)';ctx.shadowBlur=6;ctx.stroke();ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    });
    pts.forEach(p=>{ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,2.5,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fillStyle='rgba(201,168,76,0.8)';ctx.fill();});
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(201,168,76,'+(0.75+.2*Math.sin(t*1.5))+')';
    ctx.font='700 26px "Cinzel Decorative",serif';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';
    ctx.shadowColor='rgba(201,168,76,.7)';ctx.shadowBlur=18;ctx.fillText('\u03A9',CX,CY);ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }
  frame();
}

function drawWave(){
  const cv=document.getElementById('wv-cv');
  if(!cv) return;
  const W=cv.offsetWidth||200,H=36;cv.width=W*2;cv.height=H*2;cv.style.width=W+'px';cv.style.height=H+'px';
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d');ctx.scale(2,2);let t=0;
  function ecgY(x,W,t){
    const p=(x/W*3+t*.7)%1;
    if(p<.1) return -Math.sin(p/.1*Math.PI)*10;
    if(p<.12) return Math.sin((p-.1)/.02*Math.PI*2)*22;
    if(p<.16) return -Math.sin((p-.12)/.04*Math.PI)*10;
    return Math.sin((x/W*3+t*.7)*6.28*2.5)*1.5;
  }
  function frame(){
    t+=.016;ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
    ctx.beginPath();
    for(let x=0;x<=W;x+=2){const y=H/2+ecgY(x,W,t);x===0?ctx.moveTo(x,y):ctx.lineTo(x,y);}
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(63,178,127,0.7)';ctx.lineWidth=1.5;ctx.shadowColor='rgba(63,178,127,.4)';ctx.shadowBlur=5;ctx.stroke();ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }
  frame();
}

function buildAssets(){
  const tb=document.getElementById('asset-tbody');tb.innerHTML='';
  ASSETS.forEach(a=>{
    const tr=document.createElement('tr');
    tr.innerHTML=`<td><div class="at-name">${a.name}</div></td><td><span class="at-badge" style="color:${a.col};border-color:${a.col}44">${a.cat}</span></td><td style="color:var(--gold);font-family:var(--D)">${a.val}</td><td class="at-change ${a.up?'up':'dn'}">${a.ch}</td><td style="color:var(--green);font-family:var(--M);font-size:8px">ACTIVE</td>`;
    tb.appendChild(tr);
  });
}

function buildSplinter(){
  const g=document.getElementById('splinter-grid');g.innerHTML='';
  SPLINTER.forEach(s=>{
    const c=document.createElement('div');
    c.style.cssText=`border:1px solid ${s.col}33;border-left:3px solid ${s.col}77;background:linear-gradient(160deg,var(--panel),transparent);padding:14px;`;
    c.innerHTML=`<div style="font-family:var(--M);font-size:8px;letter-spacing:3px;color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:4px">${s.label}</div><div style="font-family:var(--D);font-size:22px;color:${s.col};font-weight:700">${s.pct}%</div><div style="height:2px;background:rgba(201,168,76,.08);margin-top:8px;border-radius:1px;overflow:hidden"><div style="height:100%;width:0%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,${s.col},transparent);border-radius:1px;transition:width 1.2s ease" data-w="${s.pct}"></div></div>`;
    g.appendChild(c);
    setTimeout(()=>c.querySelector('[data-w]').style.width=s.pct+'%',200);
  });
}

function buildWallet(a,b,c){
  const total=Math.round((a+b+c)*917.17);
  document.getElementById('wallet-total').innerHTML='&#937; '+total.toLocaleString();
  const g=document.getElementById('wallet-grid');g.innerHTML='';
  TOKENS.forEach(tk=>{
    const bal=parseFloat(tk.bal)||0;
    const cd=document.createElement('div');cd.className='wl-card';
    cd.style.cssText+=`border-top-color:${tk.col}`;
    cd.innerHTML=`<span class="wlc-sym" style="color:${tk.col}">${tk.sym}</span><div class="wlc-name" style="color:${tk.col}">${tk.name}</div><div class="wlc-token">${tk.token}</div><div class="wlc-bal" style="color:${bal>0?'var(--gold)':'var(--muted)'}">${bal>0?'&#937; '+bal.toLocaleString():'&#937; 0'}</div><div class="wlc-usd">${bal>0?'ACTIVE':'PENDING ACTIVATION'}</div>`;
    g.appendChild(cd);
  });
}

function buildNFTs(){
  const g=document.getElementById('nft-gallery');g.innerHTML='';
  NFTS.forEach(n=>{
    const c=document.createElement('div');c.className='nft-card';
    c.style.cssText+=`border-top:2px solid ${n.col}66`;

    g.appendChild(c);
  });
}

function buildTiers(currentTier){
  const g=document.getElementById('tier-grid');g.innerHTML='';
  TIERS.forEach((tier,i)=>{
    const active=i+1===currentTier;
    const c=document.createElement('div');c.className='tier-card'+(active?' active-tier':'');
    c.innerHTML=`<div class="tc-num" style="color:${active?tier.col:'rgba(133,131,123,0.3)'}">${tier.n}</div><div class="tc-name" style="color:${active?tier.col:'var(--muted)'}">${tier.name}</div><div class="tc-price" style="color:${active?'var(--solar)':'var(--muted)'}">${tier.price}</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:300;margin-bottom:8px">${tier.features}</div><span class="tc-badge" style="color:${active?tier.col:'var(--muted)'};border-color:${active?tier.col+'44':'rgba(133,131,123,.2)'}">${active?'CURRENT':'AVAILABLE'}</span>`;
    g.appendChild(c);
  });
}

function buildTxLog(){
  const g=document.getElementById('tx-log');g.innerHTML='';
  const items=[
    {dot:'var(--green)',desc:'Platform access -- trial period',type:'ACCESS',amount:'&#937; 0',date:'TODAY',col:'var(--green)'},
    {dot:'var(--cyan)',desc:'Academy module -- Knowledge A advancement',type:'REWARD',amount:'+&#937; 91.7',date:'TODAY',col:'var(--cyan)'},
    {dot:'var(--gold)',desc:'Monthly subscription renewal',type:'SUBSCRIPTION',amount:'READY TO ACTIVATE',date:'PENDING',col:'var(--muted)'},
    {dot:'var(--muted)',desc:'Stripe payment gateway',type:'PENDING',amount:'$0.00',date:'--',col:'var(--muted)'},
  ];
  items.forEach(it=>{
    const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='txi-item tx-item';
    d.innerHTML=`<div class="txi-dot" style="background:${it.dot}"></div><div class="txi-body"><div class="txi-desc">${it.desc}</div><span class="txi-type">${it.type}</span></div><div class="txi-amount" style="color:${it.col}">${it.amount}</div><div class="txi-date">${it.date}</div>`;
    g.appendChild(d);
  });
}

async function boot(){
  const sess=(await sb.auth.getSession()).data.session;
  if(!sess){window.location.href='/account.html';return;}
  const pr=await sb.from('profiles').select('axis_a,axis_b,axis_c,membership_tier').eq('id',sess.user.id).maybeSingle();
  const a=parseFloat(pr.data?.axis_a)||1,b=parseFloat(pr.data?.axis_b)||1,c=parseFloat(pr.data?.axis_c)||1;
  const nodes=Math.round(a*b*c*9),xp=Math.round(nodes*917.17);
  const tier=parseInt(pr.data?.membership_tier)||1;
  document.getElementById('aum-val').innerHTML='&#937; '+xp.toLocaleString();
  document.getElementById('res-phys').textContent='\u03A9 '+Math.round(xp*.6).toLocaleString();
  document.getElementById('res-dig').textContent='\u03A9 '+Math.round(xp*.4).toLocaleString();
  buildAssets();buildSplinter();buildWallet(a,b,c);buildNFTs();buildTiers(tier);buildTxLog();
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{drawVaultCube();drawWave();});
}
boot();
